Five post-sale mutation Orders were reconstructed as Orders-owned immutable netted-final-state SALE facts using their reviewed final Order/OrderItem economics: **1694 CASH**, **3456 CASH**, **2766 CARD**, **1241 CARD**, **959 CARD** cents. Two historical CARD full refunds were separately corroborated in Clover Dashboard and reconstructed as Orders-owned reversal facts: `cmpzyscpf00ceo30144uq90ug` canonical refund **1919** cents from a provider refund **2111 = 1919 Order + 192 tip**, and `cmsrnbd8t000hns01jeuuuqq6` canonical refund **2033** cents from provider refund **2082 = 2033 Order + 49 surcharge**. Dashboard time precision was minute-only, so the repair used the final millisecond of the displayed Toronto minute and preserved the precision/rule in historical metadata.  
**Fail-closed exception:** `cmsrqiohb000ons01ppnwp759` remains intentionally unresolved. Orders says FULL_REFUND **2258** and `refunded`, but Clover payment evidence was found without a corresponding provider refund. No reversal OpsEvent and no reversal Journal were created for it. This is carried as a documented accounting exception rather than inferred from mutable Order state, amendment CUID time, `updatedAt`, or amount proximity.  
**Repair mechanics:** the transactional manual SQL wrote only exact Orders-owned `OpsEvent` facts and asserted the reviewed Order/amendment state, target Journal presence/absence, zero `AUTO_ORDER*`, exact seven-fact manifest, exact idempotency-key content, and deliberate absence of the unresolved reversal. It did not create PaymentTransaction/Payments evidence, mutate Orders, write Accounting Journals/Audits directly, or alter schema/migrations. The first raw-SQL attempt failed because `OpsEvent.id` has no database-side UUID default and rolled back fully; the corrected script supplied `gen_random_uuid()` and committed exactly **7 unique OpsEvents**.  
**Production verification:** SALE preview `planHash=b47c083a0d3462845ad288756407b6d5d56af3970c961c7be9bdcfddd4fb73e9` returned **1071 READY / 0 BLOCKED**, balanced **1,954,457 / 1,954,457** with parity delta **0**; database inspection showed exactly the five reconstructed SALE facts lacked Journals. SALE replay returned HTTP 201 / `postedOrReplayed=1071` and added exactly **5 JournalEntries / 15 JournalLines / 5 CREATE Audits**, moving **1221 / 3900 / 1221 -> 1226 / 3915 / 1226**. Change preview `planHash=df9754c68573df0bdac4538b1a7c329519f97518816827c23402621308e8b4d2` returned exactly **2 READY / 0 BLOCKED / 0 unmatched**, both CARD `LEGACY_ORDER_DECLARED`, balanced **3952 / 3952**. Change replay returned HTTP 201 / `postedOrReplayed=2` and added **2 JournalEntries / 6 JournalLines / 2 CREATE Audits**, yielding final production totals **1228 / 3921 / 1228**, with legacy `AUTO_ORDER* = 0` and the unresolved reversal still absent.  
**Architecture/schema/compatibility effect:** **none**. No production source code, runtime owner boundary, public contract, dependency edge, scanner allowance, Prisma schema/migration, compatibility token, legacy writer or production Web Clover path changed. The data-specific repair must not be generalized into a runtime fallback. The recorded architecture baseline remains Foundation **2** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **11**, public SCC empty.  
**Details:** `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog; production verification used operator Clover evidence plus read-only database checks and the existing guarded Accounting replay endpoints.

### 2026-09-14 — Phase 9 Slice 6A Uber financial-reporting identity boundary contraction

**PR/SHA:** PR #2330 / final head `66722903d818ece7817806aea97a33354211fcad` / squash merge `dc9a6f80d6452a9db027ab13605e29155ab9e197`  
**State:** MERGED / PR CI #5651 GREEN  
**Readiness:** post-C2 audit is **READY FOR 6A / NOT READY FOR FULL SETTLEMENT JOURNAL CUTOVER**. The legacy flat `PlatformSettlementRecord` reconciliation still reads retired `AccountingTransaction` order revenue and therefore cannot be promoted as the 5D-era settlement path; later 6B-6D must instead reconcile provider financial evidence against canonical Journals before any settlement write cutover.  
**Result:** contracts Uber financial-report request identity back into External Channels. `UberEatsReportingPort.requestFinancialReports()` no longer accepts provider `storeUuids`; Accounting keeps only Store-local date-window calculation and the `PAYMENT_DETAILS_REPORT + FINANCE_SUMMARY_REPORT` selection. `UberFinancialReportingUseCase` now resolves existing owner-internal mappings, filters only provisioned rows, deduplicates/sorts `uberStoreId` values and supplies the provider wire store UUIDs internally. The `eats.report` deployment entitlement gate is evaluated by `UberApiConfigService` and injected by the Uber composition root, preserving the prior no-op behavior when report capability is unavailable without allowing Accounting to inspect Uber scope configuration or mappings.  
**Coverage:** Accounting characterization verifies provider capability/store resolution is delegated to the External public boundary while preserving the prior four-day rolling window and `accountingStartDate` clipping. Uber focused tests cover disabled-scope no-op, provisioned mapping filtering/deduplication and no-provisioned-store no-op. Accounting and Uber architecture guards prevent `storeUuids` from returning to the public reporting contract/Accounting scheduler and pin provider identity resolution inside External Channels.  
**Architecture/schema/compatibility effect:** no new context direction, Prisma adapter, scanner allowance, schema/migration, package dependency or compatibility path. The existing Accounting -> External Channels public edge is narrowed rather than expanded; authoritative CI #5651 preserved the recorded baseline Foundation **2** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **11**, public SCC empty. No webhook/order/menu/store-status wire behavior, settlement Journal write or production Web Clover path changes.  
**Phase-closeout verification scope:** when Phase 9 reaches consolidated active verification, enable/report the actual approved `eats.report` capability and confirm the nightly Accounting request still produces only Payment Details + Finance Summary for the provisioned store(s), remains a safe no-op without the entitlement, and does not regress OAuth/webhook/order/menu/store-status flows.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-automation.scheduler.ts,accounting-automation.characterization.spec.ts,accounting-inbox-core.architecture.spec.ts}`, `apps/api/src/integrations/ubereats/{public-api.ts,ARCHITECTURE.md,uber-store-identity-architecture.spec.ts,test/requirement-matrix.md,application/operations/uber-financial-reporting.use-cases.ts,application/operations/uber-financial-reporting.use-cases.spec.ts,infrastructure/nest/operations.wiring.ts,infrastructure/uber-api/{uber-api-config.service.ts,uber-scopes.spec.ts}}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-14 — Phase 9 Slice 6B provider settlement / reconciliation shadow policy

**PR/SHA:** PR #2331 / squash merge `2f999ec0026599159932e00ef7580fe4fa82321c`; PR CI #5656 green; post-merge CI #5657 green  
**State:** MERGED / CI GREEN  
**Readiness decision:** **READY FOR SHADOW PREVIEW / NOT READY FOR SETTLEMENT JOURNAL WRITE**. The Slice 6 read-only audit found that the double-entry settlement substrate is already mostly sufficient (Clover/Uber/Fantuan pending receivables plus commission, promotion, advertising, payment-processing fee, chargeback/adjustment and HST-recoverable accounts), but the old `PlatformSettlementRecord -> AccountingTransaction` reconciliation remains legacy-only and cannot be reused after the 5D Journal cutover. The audit also rejected direct parser-`POSTABLE` => Journal behavior because provider Sales/Tax can duplicate canonical Order revenue, payout needs its own Bank <-> pending policy, and unknown `OTHER`/`ADJUSTMENT` must fail closed. Recommended sequencing remains 6A identity contraction -> 6B shadow policy -> 6C guarded writer/cutover -> 6D legacy contraction. Production read-only inventory further established a historical Uber source-authority discontinuity: 119 manually entered Uber Orders from 2026-06-02 through 2026-07-12 already have canonical SALE Journals, while no manual Uber Orders exist from 2026-07-13 onward. Read-only Journal inspection shows 326,092 cents Uber pending, 367,850 cents Sales Revenue, 37,519 cents HST payable and 79,277 cents Sales Discounts. These Orders predate durable SALE OpsEvents, so the immutable Journal is the reliable record of what Accounting actually posted. Operator confirmation states all manual discounting represented Uber Offers/BOGO only; provider commission was never netted into the manual Order amount.  
**Authority rule:** before the real `AccountingProviderFinancialCoverage.liveOrderFactCutoverAt`, provider statements are the authoritative Uber financial source and pre-live manual Uber Order Journals are shadow reversal candidates. After the real Production integration cutover, canonical Uber Order financial facts own Sales/Tax and provider statements remain settlement/reconciliation evidence. `liveOrderFactCutoverAt` must be the actual integration cutover, not the date staff stopped manual entry. While the cutover remains null, the historical 119 manual Uber SALE Journals are non-authoritative for Accounting but are not deleted or hidden from reporting without an explicit reversal plan.  
**Shadow implementation:** adds read-only `GET /accounting/journal/provider-settlement/shadow-preview`. It reads latest provider financial document revisions, coverage metadata, active Accounting system accounts, canonical Order SALE facts and existing Journals; emits deterministic planHash, component dispositions, balanced draft Journals, missing-account/revision/cutover blockers, and pre-cutover Uber Order-Journal reversal candidates. There is no POST/replay endpoint and no Journal/Audit mutation. A pre-cutover Uber reversal is READY only when its Store-local date is covered by a READY, statement-authoritative Uber document containing postable Sales; otherwise it is blocked with `NO_READY_AUTHORITATIVE_STATEMENT_COVERAGE`, preventing a source-authority cutover from removing Order revenue before replacement statement revenue exists. Corrected provider revisions are fail-closed when a superseded revision is already posted; statement periods that cross a non-boundary live cutover are blocked instead of split by guesswork.  
**Accounting policy:** Clover Sales/Tax/Refund remain reconciliation/control evidence. Uber/Fantuan Sales and Sales Tax are statement-authoritative before live Order cutover and reconciliation-only after it. Commission -> platform commission expense; commission/processing tax -> HST recoverable; merchant Offers -> platform promotion expense; Provider Subsidy -> contra platform promotion expense; Advertising -> advertising expense; Advertising Credit -> contra advertising expense; Chargeback -> chargeback/adjustment expense. Unknown `OTHER`/`ADJUSTMENT` stays blocked; payout totals remain control-only in 6B. Operator-confirmed TIP is non-taxable Store revenue, targeted to future `account_tip_revenue` / `REVENUE`; no HST line is generated. 6B deliberately does not auto-create that system account: non-zero TIP blocks with `ACCOUNT_NOT_PROVISIONED:account_tip_revenue` until durable CoA provisioning is completed before the writer cutover.  
**Architecture/schema/compatibility effect:** no new context direction, scanner allowance, direct `PrismaService` import, schema/migration, dependency or compatibility token. The preview reuses the existing Accounting -> Orders public financial-fact edge and Accounting-owned persistence access already centralized in `AccountingOperationsService`. No production Web Clover, Uber order/webhook/menu/store-status behavior, provider ingestion wire behavior, or ledger write semantics change in 6B.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-provider-settlement.policy.ts,accounting-provider-settlement-preview.service.ts,accounting-provider-settlement.policy.spec.ts,accounting-provider-settlement-preview.service.spec.ts,accounting-operations.service.ts,accounting.controller.ts,accounting.module.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

**PR/SHA:** PR #2334 / final head `b4dbdd82eef92d184c8ecb64e5cfdd74a639f86b` / squash merge `f30275fb59620548785ba0ca58c58527f40c6ca8`

Uber statement Tips remain postable Store revenue only when the parser explicitly marks them `POSTABLE`; the non-taxable Tip policy and `account_tip_revenue` ownership are unchanged.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 Slice 6C-B atomic provider settlement / historical Uber replacement writer

**PR/SHA:** PR #2335 / final head `c993b37e8a4b5d6d8b2ee864d9845f9d4231bcaa` / squash merge `b3281410`  
**State:** MERGED / PR CI GREEN — CI #5672 green / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`  
**Result:** adds the first provider-settlement mutation endpoint, `POST /accounting/journal/provider-settlement/replay`, guarded by a caller-reviewed `expectedPlanHash`. The execution service recomputes the fresh shadow plan and writes only `READY` provider documents. Each provider document is one replacement group: its statement Journal plus every uniquely covered pre-cutover manual Uber SALE reversal are delegated together to an Accounting-owned Serializable write. The replay range must fully contain the provider document's complete period; overlap-only windows are rejected, and any related historical reversal that is not simultaneously READY blocks the statement so a monthly statement cannot be posted against only a subset/half-group of historical Orders. The group never writes `NOOP`, blocked, reconciliation-only, control-total, already-posted or already-reversed evidence.  
**Atomic/idempotency authority:** the shadow report advances to version 3 by freezing each historical SALE Journal `idempotencyKey / idempotencyHash / version / sourceFactStableId` in the plan hash. Inside the same Serializable transaction, Accounting revalidates the globally latest provider revision, exact confirmed Inbox review evidence, provider coverage snapshot, required system-account class/currency/active facts, and historical SALE Journal anchors before mutation. Deterministic idempotency remains `provider-settlement:<documentStableId>:r<revision>:v1` and `uber-pre-cutover-order-reversal:<originalJournalEntryStableId>:v1`; per-Journal idempotency hashes include the normalized replacement-group write authority. A partially persisted group fails closed instead of being silently repaired, while an identical fully persisted group is replay-safe.  
**Boundary and policy effect:** the provider execution service has no Prisma access and can mutate Journal state only through `AccountingService.createProviderSettlementReplacementGroup`; architecture guards pin that caller boundary and the GET/POST Controller surfaces. Provider financial documents/lines remain append-only revision evidence, so fresh-plan line facts plus the transaction-time latest-revision check close the supported mutation path. No Prisma schema/migration, new context edge, scanner allowance, payout writer, provider revision-correction writer, Uber cutover setter or unknown-component policy expansion is introduced. Payout remains CONTROL ONLY and unresolved Uber `OTHER / ADJUSTMENT` lines remain fail-closed.  
**Verification state:** focused execution/replacement-group tests and architecture guards were source-added without local execution; PR CI #5672 passed before merge. Production deployment/write verification remained deferred until a real confirmed provider statement/coverage plan was available; the first real Uber statement Inbox verification then exposed the PDF-ingestion compatibility defect recorded in the next entry before any settlement replay was authorized.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-provider-settlement-execution.service.ts,accounting-provider-settlement-write-authority.ts,accounting-provider-settlement-preview.service.ts,accounting-operations.service.ts,accounting.service.ts,accounting.controller.ts,accounting.module.ts,accounting-provider-settlement-execution.service.spec.ts,accounting-provider-settlement-replacement-group.spec.ts,canonical-financial-facts-boundary.architecture.spec.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 Slice 6C-B live verification compatibility hardening: PDF extraction + repeated-label amount parsing

**PR/SHA:** PR #2337 / final head `92fb66a9` / squash merge `ff97871d`  
**State:** MERGED / PR CI GREEN / POST-MERGE CI GREEN / DEPLOYED / LIVE VERIFIED — PR CI #5679 and post-merge CI #5680 green / production is running a later head that contains `ff97871d` / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`  
**Observed defect:** the first real June 2026 Uber monthly-statement upload after 6C-B produced roughly 5.1k characters dominated by punctuation/glyph codes, so the provider parser could not observe its statement markers and the Inbox fell through to ordinary expense review. The PDF is a valid text PDF using CID/`Identity-H` fonts with `ToUnicode`; the handwritten `Tj/TJ` + `FlateDecode` decoder did not resolve those font mappings. A six-document read-only spike across Uber, Fantuan, Bell, Metergy and two Fiserv statement generations showed Poppler `pdftotext` returned readable Unicode for all six, including the older Fiserv template that also defeated the handwritten extractor.  
**Source change:** `extractPdfText()` is now one bounded local Poppler pipeline (`pdftotext -enc UTF-8 -nopgbrk - -`) using stdin/stdout, a 20-second timeout and bounded output/error capture; the handwritten PDF token/font-decoding approximation is removed rather than retained as a provider-specific fallback. `Dockerfile.api` adds runtime `poppler-utils`; image OCR remains Tesseract-only. `extractAccountingPdf()` becomes async and the Inbox acquisition path awaits it.  
**Provider parser correction:** `findNamedAmount()` now scans repeated label occurrences until the adjacent token parses as money. This covers real Uber explanatory prose that mentions `Net Total` before the actual `Net Total $...` row while preserving the existing monthly-summary-only and payout-exclusion policy. The regression fixture now includes that explanatory occurrence.  
**Architecture/schema/compatibility effect:** no Prisma schema/migration, package-manifest/lockfile change, public API, provider wire contract, Journal mutation authority, context edge, scanner allowance or financial posting policy change. The architecture direct-import baseline and SCC interpretation are unchanged. Statement/expense classification, provider selection and recognition keywords remain code-owned here; operator-editable classification/provider/recognition configuration is a separately approved follow-up slice.  
**Verification state:** LIVE VERIFIED on 2026-09-15 America/Toronto after the Upload-library work allowed the stale unconfirmed copies to be permanently removed and the same June Uber statement to be acquired as a fresh source. Production persisted the new item as `PENDING_REVIEW / PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_DOCUMENT / UBER_EATS`. Recognition evidence was `accounting-provider-recognition / acct_recognition_uber_monthly_statement:v1 / SUCCESS`, matching both required keywords and both optional keywords. Canonical preview evidence was `accounting-provider-financial:v2 / SUCCESS`, period `2026-06-01..2026-06-30`, CAD, 28 normalized lines, Sales `267167`, Tax on Sales `34735`, Marketplace Fees `-61912`, Total Uber Fees `-69961`, Total Marketing Spends `-108745`, Total Amendments `-911`, and Net Total `122285` cents. Parse metadata pinned `monthlySummaryOnly=true` and `payoutSectionsExcludedFromNormalizedLines=true`; Poppler output preserved `三秦肉夹馍`, confirming the replacement extractor was actually exercised. The Inbox item remains intentionally unmaterialized until operator confirmation, so provider-settlement Journal replay is still a separate later verification gate.  
**Details:** `Dockerfile.api`, `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-pdf-extractor.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts,accounting-provider-financial.parser.ts,accounting-pdf-extractor.spec.ts,accounting-provider-financial.parser.spec.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 Accounting Inbox provider recognition configuration

**PR/SHA/State:** PR #2340 / final head `5e64b0c8` / squash merge `866966a9`; PR CI #5689 and post-merge CI #5690 green. The user-generated persistence migration was later merged at `dev@3608c2e2` as `20260916002149_add_accounting_image_binary_retention`, which creates both the recognition-rule enum/table/indexes and the separate image-retention schema. **MERGED / CI GREEN / DEPLOYED DEFAULT-RULE PATH LIVE VERIFIED** / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`  
**Result:** coarse Clover/Uber Eats/Fantuan provider/document recognition is no longer hard-coded inside the canonical provider-financial parser. Accounting now owns a bounded effective rule set with code-owned rule identities plus operator-editable required keywords, optional keywords, `ANY / ALL` optional-match mode, priority and enabled state. Defaults reproduce the current recognition behavior, the first real edit persists an override, each subsequent edit increments rule version, and every write is audited. Same-priority multi-rule matches fail closed to manual Inbox review; existing operator-reviewed Inbox items are not retroactively reclassified.

Provider API evidence remains on its explicit provider-owned path and does not consult UI recognition rules.

New Accounting endpoints list and update recognition rules, while Inbox displays recognition-only and ambiguous states distinctly from normalized provider-financial previews.

Prisma adds `AccountingProviderRecognitionMatchMode` and `AccountingProviderRecognitionRule`; the user-generated migration was merged at `dev@3608c2e2`, so there is no remaining schema-migration gate for this recognition slice.
**Verification:** the production June Uber statement matched the default `acct_recognition_uber_monthly_statement:v1` rule with all configured required/optional keywords and produced the expected `PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_DOCUMENT + UBER_EATS` suggestion before materialization. Editable-rule save/version/audit behavior is source/CI-covered but was not part of this specific live statement check.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 Accounting Inbox structured expense CSV fallback

**PR/SHA/State:** PR #2341 / final head `156854c4` / squash merge `9c358405`; PR CI #5692 and post-merge CI #5693 green. **MERGED / CI GREEN**.  
**Authorized policy change:** the user explicitly approved revising the Slice 5C-B rule that generic CSV evidence is always preserved as provider-parser-pending. CSV is treated as transport rather than business classification: Provider API CSV retains its provider-owned path; manual/Gmail CSV first evaluates existing provider recognition, and only an unambiguous miss can enter the Accounting-local structured-expense fallback.  
**Result:** adds a dependency-free, fail-closed CSV parser using semantic date/amount header families, stronger bill/invoice/charge/expense evidence or vendor/merchant/payee/description context, BOM/CRLF handling, quoted fields/escaped quotes, bounded row preview and explicit invalid-row evidence. It is generic rather than Bell-specific. Bare `Date + Amount`, malformed CSV, duplicate normalized headers, invalid dates/amounts and unknown structures remain unclassified instead of being guessed. A complete single-row match may seed the existing editable Expense suggestion; category inference is review-only and reuses generic filename/counterparty/description signals.  
**Batch safety:** multiple valid rows or any invalid row produce `requiresBatchExpenseImport=true`; no single Expense suggestion is applied, the Inbox UI previews the structured rows and disables the ordinary Expense action, and `confirmUnifiedInboxExpense()` independently rejects the batch marker even if a caller bypasses the UI. This deliberately preserves the current one-artifact/one-materialized-entity schema until a future batch-expense persistence/idempotency/audit slice is separately reviewed.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** no Prisma schema/migration, dependency manifest, provider wire contract, context direction, scanner allowance, Journal authority or posting-policy change. The direct-import baseline remains Foundation **2** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **11**, public SCC empty.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-structured-expense-csv.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts,accounting-operations.service.ts}` plus focused specs, `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/inbox/{inbox-model.ts,inbox-items-list.tsx}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 Accounting image OCR + post-confirm binary retention

**Branch/State:** PR #2342 / final head `f90f513a` / squash merge `a4c418f2` / PR CI #5699 GREEN / follow-up migration `20260916002149_add_accounting_image_binary_retention` at `dev@3608c2e2` / CI #5701 GREEN / DEPLOYED / FUNCTIONAL VERIFICATION IN PROGRESS  
**Authorized evidence-retention change:** the user approved replacing the former “raw image binary remains physically available” assumption with a post-confirm, human-approved derivative-retention lifecycle. Original source identity remains immutable: `AccountingSourceArtifact.contentHash`, original size/MIME/name and `AccountingExpenseDocument.fileHash` continue to describe the bytes actually acquired. Only the original **binary storage** may be purged after the confirmed Expense and explicit operator acceptance of the exact retained derivative. Production read-only inventory before implementation contained one PDF and zero IMAGE SourceArtifacts, so no historical image backfill is required.  
**OCR/result:** image OCR now receives the original upload rather than the lossy 2400px/q85 WebP derivative. The OCR-only Sharp pipeline remains rotate/max-3000/grayscale/normalize/sharpen/lossless PNG; normalized OCR text preserves line structure. Real OCR/preprocessing failure now records Parse `ERROR`; successful execution with empty text remains `SUCCESS + NO_READABLE_TEXT`.  
**Retention/result:** adds Accounting-owned `AccountingArtifactBinaryRetention` with recoverable `ORIGINAL_PRESENT -> CANDIDATE_READY -> PURGE_PENDING -> COMPRESSED_ONLY` semantics. Confirmed image Expenses may repeatedly generate `SPACE_SAVER`, `BALANCED`, `HIGH_QUALITY` or `NEAR_ORIGINAL` WebP candidates, compare original/candidate dimensions and size, and replace only the unaccepted candidate. Acceptance re-verifies original/candidate SHA-256, durably records retained derivative/operator/policy evidence, then deletes the original binary and finalizes only afterward; a crash remains safely retryable from `PURGE_PENDING`. Choosing to keep the original discards the candidate and leaves source storage unchanged. Confirmed image attachments use stable `/accounting/inbox/artifacts/:artifactStableId/content` URLs, which resolve original before acceptance and retained WebP after purge without rewriting already-posted Expense/Transaction evidence. The Inbox also exposes a persistent image-optimization queue for `ORIGINAL_PRESENT`, `CANDIDATE_READY`, and `PURGE_PENDING`: refresh/browser close cannot lose the entry point, an existing candidate is reopened without implicit recompression, and purge-pending recovery can be resumed later but not rolled back after deletion acceptance.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** retention writes live in a TransactionClient-only Accounting writer and are orchestrated without another `PrismaService` boundary. Initial PR CI #5694 correctly rejected a third direct Accounting -> architecture-foundation upload-path import. The same-scope fix introduced one Accounting-local storage-path adapter and routed acquisition/controller/retention through it, contracting that pair **2 -> 1**; `tools/architecture/context-baseline.json` was tightened accordingly. CI #5699 confirmed Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty. No new package dependency, external/public context edge, provider wire behavior, Journal authority or posting policy. Prisma added `AccountingArtifactBinaryRetentionState` and `AccountingArtifactBinaryRetention`; the user-generated migration `20260916002149_add_accounting_image_binary_retention` subsequently merged at `dev@3608c2e2`, passed CI #5701, and was deployed before functional verification continued.  
**Details:** `apps/api/prisma/schema.prisma`, `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-image-ocr.ts,accounting-receipt-image.ts,accounting-image-retention.*,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts,accounting-inbox-query.ts,accounting-operations.service.ts,accounting.controller.ts,accounting.module.ts}`, focused specs including the retention queue query, `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/inbox/{expense-review-panel.tsx,image-retention-panel.tsx,image-retention-queue.tsx,inbox-model.ts,page.tsx}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 manual upload library + evidence-state permissions

**Branch/State:** PR #2344 / final head `00cf803a` / squash merge `376cea32` / PR CI #5709 GREEN / MERGED / CI GREEN / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`  
**Authorized retention-policy change:** unconfirmed `MANUAL_UPLOAD` evidence is now operator-managed temporary evidence rather than permanently immutable evidence. `Abandon` is non-destructive: it marks eligible `PENDING_REVIEW` / `QUARANTINED` / `ERROR` items `DISCARDED` while keeping the file/source records, but does not currently restore them to active review. `Delete permanently` is a separate irreversible action that removes eligible unconfirmed manual-upload binary plus its related Inbox/parse/image-retention/pending-or-discarded Expense persistence and associated audit rows. The user explicitly authorized complete erasure rather than a tombstone for never-confirmed manual uploads. `CONFIRMED`, Gmail/Provider API-owned, Provider Financial, transaction-backed, or otherwise protected duplicate-anchor evidence remains non-deletable.  
**Duplicate/result:** manual upload responses now surface real Inbox status. A byte-identical manual re-upload reports `DUPLICATE` instead of the generic success message; the duplicate metadata row remains visible in the Upload library, but the just-written second raw binary is released immediately and `storedUrl` is cleared. If an original manual source is permanently erased, only unconfirmed manual Duplicate dependents may be removed with it; any Gmail/Provider/protected dependent blocks deletion.  
**UI/API effect:** Accounting Inbox adds a persistent Upload library for all manual uploads with filename/kind/size/time/status, duplicate linkage, retained-file access and server-derived `canDiscard` / `canPermanentDelete` permissions. Pending/quarantined/error rows expose **Abandon**; duplicate/discarded rows may be permanently deleted when eligible; confirmed rows are view-only. `GET /accounting/inbox/manual-uploads` supplies the library and `DELETE /accounting/inbox/manual-uploads/:inboxItemStableId/permanent` owns hard deletion.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** hard-delete persistence is isolated in an Accounting-owned `Prisma.TransactionClient` writer; filesystem cleanup remains in the existing Accounting acquisition/service boundary. No Prisma schema/migration, dependency, context edge, scanner allowance, provider wire contract, Journal authority or posting-policy change. Focused query/writer/acquisition tests cover duplicate binary release, permission projection, confirmed/protected delete rejection and complete unconfirmed cleanup.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-upload-library.writer.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts,accounting-inbox-query.ts,accounting-inbox-expense.writer.ts,accounting-operations.service.ts,accounting.controller.ts}` plus focused specs; `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/inbox/{manual-upload-library.tsx,inbox-model.ts,inbox-items-list.tsx,page.tsx}`; `docs/architecture/phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md`; this worklog.

### 2026-09-15 — Phase 9 Accounting manual-upload filename encoding hardening

**PR/SHA/State:** PR #2346 / final head `ff804de9` / squash merge `03efff85`; PR CI #5717 green. **MERGED / CI GREEN / DEPLOYED / PRODUCTION VERIFIED** / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`  
**Production verification:** on 2026-09-16 America/Toronto, the operator confirmed the manual-upload filename issue is resolved in production: newly uploaded Chinese filenames no longer surface with the prior UTF-8-as-Latin-1 mojibake. The intentionally unsupported historical mojibake rows remain outside compatibility scope and may be permanently deleted through the Upload library.  
**Observed defect/root cause:** production read-only inspection showed manual PDF names stored as `6 2026_SanQ Roujiamo ä¸ç§¦èå¤¹é¦.pdf`, while the intended browser filename was `6 2026_SanQ Roujiamo 三秦肉夹馍.pdf`. The byte pattern is UTF-8 multipart parameter data interpreted as Latin-1 by the Multer/Busboy filename boundary; PDF extraction, PostgreSQL UTF-8 storage and Web rendering are not the corruption source.  
**Source/result:** manual intake now normalizes only the Multer `originalname` boundary before file detection, persistence, provider recognition and generic review parsing. The decoder is conservative: already-correct Unicode is preserved, ASCII is unchanged, invalid UTF-8 Latin-1 input is left untouched, and only a valid Latin-1-byte -> UTF-8 round trip is repaired. Gmail and Provider API filenames bypass this normalization. Existing stored mojibake rows are intentionally not compatibility-normalized or rewritten; the current test uploads will be permanently deleted through the Upload library.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** the filename-normalization helper stays entirely inside Accounting manual acquisition. No Prisma schema/migration, dependency, public API, context edge, scanner allowance, Journal authority or posting-policy change. Focused tests cover the real Chinese filename pattern, already-correct Unicode/ASCII/Latin-1 safety, and persistence/parser normalization.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-upload-filename.ts,accounting-upload-filename.spec.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.spec.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Accounting Inbox receipt-image OCR quality hardening

**Branch/State:** `phase9-accounting-image-ocr-quality` / PR #2349 / final head `eb784b99` / squash merge `74c484fa` / CI #5729 **GREEN / DEPLOYED / QUALITY GATE VERIFIED / OCR RECOGNITION STILL FAILED**. The branch was recreated from the then-latest `origin/dev` after the previous live-verification branch had already been squash-merged through PR #2348, so the OCR PR did not reintroduce the old branch history. Production re-upload of the 383,073-byte `WechatIMG153.jpg` correctly failed closed as `AccountingParseStatus.ERROR` with `Accounting image OCR produced low-quality text (score 265)` instead of persisting another successful garbage parse, but it did not yet meet the real-receipt recognition bar.  
**Observed defect/root cause:** production read-only evidence for the real Foody Mart long JPEG showed the image reached Tesseract from original SourceArtifact bytes and the OCR process returned success, but persisted generic review contained only ~229 characters of garbage with no date/tax/total. The prior OCR preparation constrained both dimensions to 3000px without enlargement, so a tall receipt could be downscaled by height until its narrow text became smaller; it did not trim dark surrounding background, had only one grayscale/normalize/sharpen candidate, always enabled `eng+chi_sim`, forced `--psm 6`, and accepted any non-crashing Tesseract output as successful evidence. The failure is therefore OCR preparation/segmentation/quality acceptance, not retention compression or downstream classification/parser policy.  
**Source/result:** `accounting-image-ocr.ts` now treats receipt length as geometry rather than as a Foody-specific template. A bounded 1000px analysis preview estimates the effective paper/content width only for segmentation decisions; OCR still reads regions derived from the original uploaded bytes. Short receipts may remain one region, while long/narrow receipts are split into ordered overlapping vertical regions before OCR so additional receipt length increases region count instead of shrinking the entire document. Receipt-focused regions apply EXIF rotation, background trim, width-oriented bounded resize/upscale, grayscale/normalize/sharpen and lossless PNG, then run English-only Tesseract with `--psm 4` / `--dpi 300`; region text is reassembled in source order with deterministic overlap-line deduplication. If the primary contrast strategy is weak, a second English strategy adds binary thresholding; only if quality remains weak does an untrimmed `eng+chi_sim / --psm 6` fallback run. Winner selection remains deterministic from printable/normal-token ratios, money/date patterns, receipt signals and line structure with explicit noise penalties; text length is not the selection criterion. Empty OCR maps to `SUCCESS + NO_READABLE_TEXT` only when attempted OCR processes complete normally; execution failure remains an error, and non-empty low-quality text now fails through the existing acquisition error path instead of persisting `SUCCESS + garbage`.  
**Resource/retention boundary:** original upload remains the only OCR source. Input remains under the existing 20MB / 80M-pixel image policy. Each OCR region targets 1800px working width and is capped at 5000px height / 9M pixels / 24MB; one strategy may contain at most 10 ordered regions / 64MB prepared buffers, with 180px working overlap. If preserving text scale would require more than 10 regions, preprocessing fails closed instead of globally shrinking the receipt. Up to three strategies execute progressively; each Tesseract region process keeps the 20s timeout and one strategy's combined output remains capped at 512KB. No fixed receipt/terminal split, vendor-specific receipt length, arbitrary-angle deskew or perspective correction is added; Sharp cannot safely supply the latter two automatically, and current evidence does not justify a new dependency. The post-confirm image-retention lifecycle, Upload library, PDF/CSV/provider recognition, Journal/revenue/expense-confirm semantics are unchanged.  
**Focused regression coverage authored:** synthetic/de-identified tests now use generic merchant/item names and cover dark-background crop, a high-aspect-ratio very-long receipt that must form multiple bounded regions, fail-closed behavior beyond the region budget, ordered overlap merge/deduplication, clear-receipt non-lossy preparation, receipt-quality scoring versus longer garbage, deterministic ties, English-first/mixed-language fallback, low-quality rejection, empty OCR and line preservation. Existing acquisition tests continue to pin original-byte ownership and Parse `ERROR` on OCR exceptions; existing retention writer/query/architecture tests continue to pin the retention lifecycle. No terminal PAN/card number is stored in test data. Per repository policy these tests were authored but not run locally; GitHub Actions is the validation gate after remote submission.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** no Prisma schema/migration, package/system dependency, public API, cross-context direction, architecture scanner allowance, provider wire contract, Journal authority or posting policy change. CI #5729 confirmed the direct-import baseline remained Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty.  
**Production verification after merge/deploy:** the Foody Mart image remains one production regression sample rather than a recognition template: re-upload it and require human-readable OCR with most of `FOODY MART`, `2026/09/08`, `42.38`, `Sub Total`, `HST`, `Total after Tax`, and `Debit` / `Debit Card`; merchant must not be predominantly garbage, date must not fall back to upload date, and total must resolve to `$42.38`. Item amounts `11.32 / 23.07 / 7.99` are second-tier acceptance. Also verify a materially longer receipt: OCR must increase ordered region count instead of globally shrinking text, preserve line order across overlaps and stay within resource guards. If skew/perspective remains the blocker after this pipeline, review a new imaging dependency separately rather than weakening the quality gate.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-image-ocr.ts,accounting-image-ocr.spec.ts}`; existing ownership/error guards in `accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.spec.ts` and retention guards remain unchanged; `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Slice 6C-B.1 Uber settlement canonical component taxonomy hardening

**Branch/State:** `phase9-6c-b1-uber-settlement-components` / PR #2351 / final head `250875d1` / squash merge `a83cdce5` / CI #5736 **GREEN / MIGRATION MERGED / PRODUCTION MATERIALIZATION VERIFIED**. User-generated migration `20260916060832_slice6c_b1_uber_settlement_components` merged at `03df1c19`.  
**Observed blocker:** the real June 2026 Uber monthly statement is parser-verified but contains four non-zero lines that the existing 6C-B settlement policy intentionally blocks as generic `OTHER / ADJUSTMENT`: `Other Charges -2`, `Tax on offer spends -7633`, `Tax on Net Ad Spends -4880`, and `Net Tax On Chargeback -105` cents. These amounts participate in `Net Total = 122285`, so they cannot be hidden as control/reconciliation-only evidence merely to make the shadow plan READY. Production read-only inspection also confirmed 83 June manual Uber Orders with 83 canonical SALE Journals, zero deleted/invalid anchors, and zero existing pre-cutover Uber reversal Journals; the replacement-side blocker is therefore component taxonomy, not historical Journal readiness.  
**Source/result:** `AccountingFinancialComponent` adds the observed canonical identities `PLATFORM_OTHER_FEE`, `PLATFORM_OTHER_FEE_TAX`, `ADVERTISING_TAX`, and `CHARGEBACK_TAX`. The Uber parser advances to version `3`: `Other Charges` / its tax companion map to platform-other-fee components; `Tax on Net Ad Spends` becomes `ADVERTISING_TAX / INPUT_TAX`; `Net Tax On Chargeback` becomes `CHARGEBACK_TAX / SALES_TAX`; and `Tax on offer spends` reuses `SALES_TAX / SALES_TAX`. The latter preserves the existing authority split because Uber Order ingestion already freezes promotion-adjusted tax: pre-cutover statement tax reduction remains postable, while post-cutover `SALES_TAX` remains Orders-authoritative/reconciliation-only.  
**Posting policy:** no new CoA account is created. Platform other fee uses existing `account_general_operating_expense`; platform-other-fee tax and advertising tax use `account_hst_recoverable`; chargeback tax uses `account_hst_payable`. Generic non-zero `OTHER` and generic `ADJUSTMENT` remain `UNMAPPED_PROVIDER_COMPONENT`. A production-shaped June regression pins `account_uber_pending = Dr 122285`, provider draft parity `294164 / 294164`, Sales Revenue `Cr 267167`, HST Payable `Cr 26997`, HST Recoverable `Dr 12927`, and the existing commission/promotion/advertising/chargeback/general-expense account totals.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** Accounting ownership, GET shadow/POST execution boundary, expected-plan-hash authority, Serializable replacement group, payout control-only policy, provider wire contracts, context directions and scanner allowances are unchanged. Direct-import baseline remains Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty. The persisted enum expansion is now represented by user-generated migration `20260916060832_slice6c_b1_uber_settlement_components`, whose SQL only adds the four approved enum values.  
**Production verification:** the real June statement was confirmed on 2026-09-16 and materialized as revision `1` with parser `accounting-provider-financial:v3`, 28 normalized lines, correct mapped components and Uber coverage starting `2026-06-01`; `financialCompleteThrough`, `liveOrderFactCutoverAt`, and `orderDetailCoverageFrom` remain null as intended. Confirmation created zero provider-settlement/reversal Journals. The remaining gate is the full-period `2026-06-01 -> 2026-07-01` Uber shadow preview: require one READY provider document, zero blocked provider documents, provider draft `294164 / 294164`, Uber Pending `122285`, all 83 June historical reversals READY, repeated deterministic `planHash`, and still zero settlement/reversal Journals before any separately authorized POST replay.  
**Details:** `apps/api/prisma/schema.prisma`, `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-provider-financial.parser.ts,accounting-provider-financial.parser.spec.ts,accounting-provider-financial.service.spec.ts,accounting-provider-settlement.policy.ts,accounting-provider-settlement.policy.spec.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Accounting Inbox real-receipt OCR follow-up

**Branch/State:** `phase9-accounting-image-ocr-real-receipt` / **SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / REMOTE SUBMISSION IN PROGRESS / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`**. The follow-up starts from latest `origin/dev` after the Uber settlement source batch ended, so it does not reuse or overwrite that feature branch.  
**Production evidence/root cause:** the deployed #2349 quality gate correctly rejected the real re-upload with score `265`, proving fail-closed behavior but not recognition quality. Inspection of the exact 1080x1920 source shows a narrow light thermal receipt on a dark textured surface with broad side background, faded print, and uneven shadow/reflection; severe perspective is not the demonstrated blocker. The first hardening preview estimated effective paper width for vertical segmentation but receipt-focused OCR still extracted full photograph width and depended on Sharp `trim()`, while the binary pass used one global threshold (`180`). Those assumptions are too weak for textured background plus uneven illumination.  
**Source/result:** the bounded <=1000px grayscale analysis preview now derives an Otsu split and one-dimensional bright-paper column support. Only a high-confidence width/support/inside-vs-outside run becomes a horizontally cropped original-byte source region; uncertain geometry falls back to full width, and the existing full-frame mixed-language candidate remains available. The second English receipt pass replaces fixed global thresholding with a sliding-window local mean threshold using the existing bounded segment pixels; OCR normalization repairs common comma/spaced two-decimal artifacts, receipt scoring recognizes named dates already supported downstream, and low-quality errors include bounded per-strategy score/normal/noise/money/date/signal diagnostics. No merchant string, fixed Foody coordinate or fixed receipt length enters recognition policy.  
**Regression/resource boundary:** synthetic fixtures now include deterministic dark texture, assert actual horizontal source isolation when confidence is strong, preserve full-width behavior for clear full-frame receipts, and pin local threshold behavior under two illumination levels. Existing long-receipt segmentation/fail-closed limits, original-byte ownership, deterministic winner/overlap merge, OCR-error persistence and image-retention guards remain unchanged. The local threshold uses a width-sized `Uint32Array` and one output buffer rather than a full-image integral matrix; existing 9M-pixel / 24MB region, 10-region / 64MB candidate and Tesseract timeout/output guards remain authoritative. The real uploaded receipt and terminal/card data are not committed as test fixtures.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** remains wholly inside Accounting and reuses Sharp/Tesseract already in the repository. No Prisma schema/migration, package/system dependency, public API, cross-context direction, architecture scanner allowance, PDF/CSV/provider parsing, Upload-library/retention lifecycle, Journal authority or posting-policy change. Expected direct-import baseline remains Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty; GitHub Actions remains the authoritative validation gate after user review.  
**Production verification after merge/deploy:** re-upload the same real source and require human-readable OCR with most of `FOODY MART`, the Sep 08 2026 / `2026/09/08` date, `$42.38`, `Sub Total`, `HST`, `Total after Tax`, and `Debit` / `Debit Card`; date must not fall back to upload day and total must resolve to `$42.38`. Then verify one materially longer receipt to confirm ordered multi-region scaling rather than whole-document shrinking. If it still fails, use the new per-strategy diagnostics before considering any separately authorized deskew/perspective dependency.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-image-ocr.ts,accounting-image-ocr.spec.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

Production currently has 83/83 valid June SALE anchors, zero target settlement/reversal Journals and no June period close.  
**Source/result:** the Web settlement page now exposes the previously hidden plan hash at the top with copy support and adds a dedicated replay-gate component rather than expanding the already-large page container. A pure UI policy fails closed unless one exact provider document is READY, provider/reversal counts match their arrays, every reversal is READY and uniquely covered, review/materialized linkage and coverage are present, provider scope/full period match, provider/reversal amounts and draft-line sums are balanced/consistent, a provider pending line exists and the hash is valid SHA-256. READY shows the exact provider/reversal/total Journal summary and provider-pending net. Arming requires the displayed period-derived phrase (June: `REPLAY JUNE 2026`) plus an explicit acknowledgement of the exact Journal count. Any new plan hash clears the armed confirmation.  
**Unknown-outcome/reconciliation rule:** the final action posts only the current preview range/provider/hash to the existing `/accounting/journal/provider-settlement/replay`. Regardless of POST response, the UI immediately performs a fresh shadow GET. Only `ALREADY_POSTED` plus an all-`ALREADY_REVERSED` historical set is treated as verified completion; transport/refresh uncertainty becomes `UNKNOWN / RECONCILIATION REQUIRED` and disables another replay in the current page session. The UI offers no undo and never automatically retries a possibly successful write.  
**Regression/architecture effect:** production-shaped Web policy tests pin June as 1 provider + 83 reversals = 84 Journals, provider `294164 / 294164`, reversals `294474 / 294474`, Uber Pending `122285`, and terminal post-state behavior; negative coverage includes a blocked reversal and unexpected second provider document. No Prisma/schema/migration, package dependency, server writer, provider parser/mapping, CoA, provider wire, source-authority rule, context direction or scanner allowance changes. Expected baseline remains Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty.  
**Post-merge production verification:** deployed UI reproduced the real June statement plan as `READY` with visible `planHash = d31ea12be4baf27785438c54710015ba508893a390d322d0a5c9ad9118396fce`, 84 prospective Journals (1 provider + 83 reversals), provider `294164 / 294164`, reversals `294474 / 294474`, and Uber Pending `122285`. A final read-only pre-write audit reconfirmed 83/83 valid unique SALE anchors, no existing settlement/reversal targets, no June period close, `AUTO_ORDER* = 0`, one latest CONFIRMED parser-v3 statement with 28 lines, and valid settlement account prerequisites; API logs showed the fresh shadow GET and no replay POST.  
**Replay completion:** the operator later executed the real June replay through the deployed strong-confirmation gate. Read-only production verification immediately afterward found exactly **1** active `provider-settlement:*` Journal balanced at `294164 / 294164` cents plus exactly **83** active `uber-pre-cutover-order-reversal:*` Journals balanced at `294474 / 294474` cents. `PlatformSettlementRecord` remained at **0** rows and `AccountingTransaction` at **0** rows. June settlement posting/reversal replacement is therefore production-verified; later provider periods remain independent evidence/cutover work and must not be inferred from this June completion.  
**Details:** `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/settlements/{page.tsx,settlement-model.ts,settlement-replay-gate.tsx,settlement-replay-policy.ts,settlement-replay-policy.test.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Expense Inbox ownership + CAD functional-currency hardening

**PR/SHA/State:** PR #2359 / final head `db0fe8ba` / squash merge `f77b66d0`; PR CI #5763 and post-merge CI #5764 green. **MERGED / CI GREEN / LEGACY DATA CLEANUP VERIFIED / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`**.  
**Ownership/UI result:** evidence-backed receipt/invoice review remains in Accounting Inbox through final category allocation and confirmation. The product-by-product quick-classification calculator moves from the Expenses page into the Inbox expense-review panel; item rows remain browser-only working state and aggregate into durable category totals, so no SKU/product-name accounting model is introduced. Expenses is reframed as confirmed-expense visibility plus an explicit no-evidence manual-entry form; uploaded-document review/calculation is no longer duplicated there. Missing recognized dates no longer silently appear as the upload/current date in the Inbox review form.  
**Currency policy:** SanQ bookkeeping remains CAD functional currency. Generic text extraction records only explicit source-currency evidence (`CAD/USD/EUR/GBP`) and fails missing/multiple explicit currencies to `UNKNOWN/AMBIGUOUS`; source subtotal/tax/total remain recognition evidence. Inbox separately displays source-document amounts/currency and the final CAD booked total/category allocation. Foreign evidence such as a USD Cloudflare invoice does not prefill USD minor units into CAD booking fields; the operator enters the actual CAD charge. Confirmed ExpenseDocument/AccountingTransaction currency is explicitly CAD, and non-CAD payment accounts are rejected. Existing `extractionJson` stores reviewed source currency plus booked CAD subtotal/tax/total, avoiding a multi-currency ledger/schema expansion.  
**Atomic confirmation:** new `confirmUnifiedInboxExpense()` no longer materializes a pending ExpenseDocument in a separate transaction. One Serializable Accounting transaction revalidates Inbox/classification, period/start-date, categories and CAD payment account, then creates the already-CONFIRMED ExpenseDocument, category Transactions and Audit rows and marks the Inbox item CONFIRMED/materialized. The old materialization entry point is removed from `AccountingOperationsService`; no current runtime caller remains. Characterization covers foreign source evidence with CAD booking and non-CAD account rejection.  
**Legacy data cleanup:** the pre-write audit found exactly **7** pre-refactor `GMAIL / PENDING_REVIEW` ExpenseDocuments, each with zero active AccountingTransactions and no current Inbox materialization link, plus a duplicate user-created random-ID `CIBC` BANK account with zero Journal/Transaction/Expense references. After merge, the operator executed the separately reviewed condition-guarded SQL. Follow-up read-only verification returned old Gmail documents `0`, old CIBC `0`, and canonical `account_primary_bank` valid count `1`.  
**Architecture/schema effect:** no Prisma schema/migration, package dependency, Textract integration, context edge, scanner allowance, Journal authority, provider-settlement policy or revenue-posting change. Native-text PDF/CSV/email recognition remains local. CI preserved Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-pdf-extractor.ts,accounting-operations.service.ts,accounting-inbox-query.ts}` plus focused specs; `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/{expenses,inbox}`; `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Textract Expense Recognition Provider

**PR/SHA/State:** PR #2360 / final head `cea260e0` / squash merge `8cd2168f`; PR CI #5767 and post-merge CI #5768 green. **MERGED / CI GREEN / DEPLOYED / REAL RECEIPT SMOKE VERIFIED / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`**. The user explicitly authorized the new npm dependency before source modification.  
**Recognition ownership/routing:** Accounting gained an internal AWS Textract `AnalyzeExpense` adapter behind opt-in `ACCOUNTING_TEXTRACT_ENABLED` (default false). Receipt images submit a transient auto-oriented, high-confidence horizontally cropped, bounded JPEG; original SourceArtifact bytes remain unchanged and still feed the existing Tesseract fallback when Textract/preparation fails. Native-text PDFs remain Poppler/provider-parser first and do not call AWS; only an empty-text scanned PDF may try synchronous Textract, with multi-page/oversize cases failing back to the existing review path rather than adding S3/async infrastructure. CSV and email-body parsing remain local. Image-recognized text now runs provider-financial recognition before generic expense suggestion, matching the established PDF ordering.  
**Financial safety:** structured Textract `SUBTOTAL/TAX/TOTAL` are editable recognition candidates and gain arithmetic consistency diagnostics; `AMOUNT_PAID` is evidence-only and cannot override total. Provider-inferred currency is evidence-only; only explicit source text promotes `CAD/USD/EUR/GBP` into `sourceCurrency`. `LineItemGroups` never become durable SKU rows; only count/price-sum/reconciliation diagnostics survive, so confirmed Expense facts remain category-level. The persisted evidence is the bounded Accounting projection rather than raw AWS JSON. Generic Inbox parser version advances to `2` for the new recognition metadata/routing semantics.  
**Dependency/architecture effect:** adds explicitly authorized `@aws-sdk/client-textract` to `apps/api`; the user regenerated `pnpm-lock.yaml` with pnpm before PR validation. No Prisma schema/migration, public API, image-retention writer, Journal authority, Expense posting policy, context import or scanner allowance change. CI preserved Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **6**, total **10**, public SCC empty.  
**Production smoke:** after IAM `textract:AnalyzeExpense` permission and runtime enablement, the known Foody `$42.38` receipt produced `ocrEngine = AWS_TEXTRACT`, a cropped `624x1920` submission, `subtotal/tax/total = 42.38/0/42.38`, three detected PRICE values summing to `42.38`, and `lineItemsReconcileToSubtotal = true`. Textract still suggested incorrect `USD`, but `sourceCurrency` correctly remained null/UNKNOWN. The same smoke exposed a date-arbitration bug: candidates included both `2026-09-01` and `2026-09-08`, while full-text parsing matched the wrong candidate and bypassed the intended conflict fail-close.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-textract-expense-recognition.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts,accounting-image-ocr.ts}` plus focused specs, `apps/api/package.json`, `docker-compose.yml`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Textract date-arbitration hardening

**PR/SHA/State:** PR #2362 / final head `25b64577` / squash merge `93322a61`; PR CI #5775 and post-merge CI #5776 green. **MERGED / CI GREEN / DEPLOYED / REAL RECEIPT VERIFIED / NO LOCAL TEST EXECUTION PER `AGENTS.md`**.  
**Root cause/fix:** the prior date guard only failed closed when conflicting Textract dates existed **and** the generic full-text date did not match any candidate. On the real Foody receipt, generic parsing selected the wrong `2026-09-01`, that value was also one Textract candidate, and the function returned it before noticing a second normalized date `2026-09-08`. The hardened arbitration checks the distinct normalized provider-date set first: more than one date always returns null; one provider date conflicts with a different generic date also returns null; only a single agreeing provider date, or generic-only date when Textract yields no normalized date, may auto-populate.  
**Regression/architecture effect:** a focused regression reproduces the real candidate set (`26/09/08`, `2026/09/01`, `Sep 08 2026`) with full-text parsing that sees the wrong date first and requires `extraction.date = null` while preserving the correct `42.38` total. The same batch improves Inbox quick-classification ergonomics without changing persistence: Enter in an amount field moves to the next existing row, or appends a new row only from the last non-zero amount; appended rows inherit the prior category and tax mode and receive amount focus automatically. No dependency, Prisma/schema, public API, retention, Journal, currency, amount, OCR preparation, context edge or scanner baseline change.  
**Production verification:** the redeployed Foody `$42.38` receipt remained `AWS_TEXTRACT`, retained `42.38 / 0 / 42.38`, three PRICE values summing to `42.38`, non-canonical Textract `USD`, and the same conflicting date candidates, while the latest ParseRun correctly persisted `date = null`.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-textract-expense-recognition.{ts,spec.ts}`, `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/inbox/expense-review-panel.tsx`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-16 — Phase 9 Textract review-only line-item hints

After deployment, re-upload/reparse the known Foody `$42.38` receipt and require parser v3 with three hints and automatic calculator prefill; confirm aggregation stays disabled until source currency `CAD`, then verify category/tax edits collapse only to category totals. Also re-test the `$105.35` receipt: if line-item prices still fail reconciliation, it must remain manual rather than auto-prefilling unreliable rows.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/{accounting-textract-expense-recognition.ts,accounting-inbox-acquisition.service.ts}` plus focused specs; `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/inbox/{inbox-model.ts,inbox-model.spec.ts,expense-review-panel.tsx}`; `docs/architecture/{phase-9-accounting-reporting-analytics.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

Opening evidence freezes permanently once any run has reached APPROVED, using durable `approvedAt` even after VOID. B3 also corrects B2 headline pensionable/insurable earnings so reviewed CPP-exempt/EI-non-insurable runs contribute zero corresponding earnings basis to YTD.

### 2026-09-18 — Phase 9 Slice 8P-D4-D Payroll accrual-date semantic correction

**Branch/State:** PR #2399 / final head `143413ef` / squash `169017df` / **MERGED / CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION**. PR CI #5915 and post-merge push CI #5916 passed all required API/Web/Architecture gates.  
**Scope/result:** before the first production Payroll fact, separates Payroll financial accrual timing from statutory payday timing. `payroll.run.accrual.v1` now carries server-owned `accrualDate = PayrollRun.periodEnd`; wage expense, employer contribution expense, vacation accrual and the matching payroll liabilities use that date as Journal `occurredAt`. `payDate` remains frozen separately for statutory calculation, YTD/config selection and CRA remittance-period policy. The inverse `payroll.run.reversal.v1` uses the same `accrualDate`, so accrual and reversal are governed by the same Accounting period lock.  
**Settlement semantics:** employee net-pay Journal remains on actual employee-payment `paymentDate`; CRA remittance Journal remains on actual CRA payment date. No statutory/YTD/remittance formula is changed. MONTHLY June work paid in July therefore recognizes expense/liability on June 30 while retaining the July payday. BIWEEKLY remains supported; MVP cross-month biweekly accrues wholly on its period end, with any future cross-month allocation explicitly deferred.  
**Authority hardening:** `AccountingJournalService` re-reads both PayrollRun `payDate` and `periodEnd` and rejects an authority whose derived accrual date drifts. Focused Journal/period-lock characterization uses deliberately different payday and accrual dates, and architecture guards prohibit returning to `occurredAt = payDate`.  
**Architecture/migration:** no Prisma schema/model/column/constraint, migration, package/lockfile, public context edge, scanner allowance or direct-import baseline change. Expected baseline remains Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **4**, total **8**, public SCC empty pending authoritative CI after operator review.

### 2026-09-18 — Phase 9 8P-E deployment prerequisite: API Docker builder heap

**Branch/State:** `fix/api-docker-build-heap` from `origin/dev@169017df` / **LOCAL SOURCE REVIEW PENDING / NO MIGRATION / NO LOCAL CI CLAIMED**.  
**Observed failure:** production-VM `docker compose build` reached `nest build` successfully after shared-library builds and Prisma generation, then Node 20 aborted near a ~972 MiB heap limit with `Ineffective mark-compacts near heap limit / JavaScript heap out of memory`. GitHub CI for the same source had already passed API build/strict/Jest, isolating the failure to the small deployment VM's builder memory envelope rather than a TypeScript/Payroll source failure.  
**Fix:** scope `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=1536` only to the API Docker builder's `pnpm --filter api build` layer. The final runner does not inherit the setting, so production API runtime memory behavior is unchanged. The existing duplicate shared-library/Prisma work inside the Docker build is intentionally left untouched in this hotfix.  
**Architecture/migration:** Docker build infrastructure only; no Prisma/schema/migration, package/lockfile, API runtime contract, public context edge, scanner allowance or direct-import baseline change.  
**Verification after merge:** rebuild the API image on the production VM; because the VM has ~2 GiB RAM and 4 GiB swap, prefer API-first/sequential image build if full parallel `docker compose build` causes host-level memory pressure. Then continue 8P-E controlled Payroll verification.

### 2026-09-19 — Phase 9 8P-E Payroll production UX forward fixes

**Branch/State:** PR #2403 / final head `41c358f0` / squash `c8f7a6d8` / **MERGED / PR CI #5930 GREEN / POST-MERGE CI #5931 GREEN / DEPLOYED / PARTIALLY PRODUCTION VERIFIED / NO MIGRATION**.  
**Production evidence:** the first controlled POSTED PayrollRun exported a correct `PAY_STATEMENT_V1`: employee/employer identity, June 2026 period, 2026-07-07 payday, earnings/deductions/net pay and YTD facts matched frozen PayrollRun evidence, and `PAYROLL_PAY_STATEMENT_EXPORT` audit evidence was written. Visual review found the non-editable-source footer alone on page 2. A later controlled employee-create test returned HTTP 201 twice and persisted two active `Yuqiao Song` rows because the Web UI refreshed the list but preserved the previously selected employee and left the submitted name fields populated, making the successful create look like a no-op and allowing an accidental repeat submission.  
**Fix:** keep all frozen Payroll facts and pay-statement semantics unchanged; render the ten YTD facts as a two-column five-row grid so the statement/footer fit one LETTER page, and assert one PDF page. For employee creation, consume the server-returned `PayrollEmployee`, wait for the existing payroll refresh, select that exact `employeeStableId`, and clear legal/display name fields after success; the existing busy gate continues to block concurrent submission. A Web source regression guard pins the post-create selection/clear behavior and refresh-before-return ordering.  
**Architecture/migration:** internal Payroll PDF renderer + Accounting Web adapter UX corrections only; no Prisma/schema/migration, employee-name uniqueness rule, package/lockfile, API contract, public context edge, scanner allowance, direct-import baseline or financial-semantic change. Same-name employees remain valid business records. Expected baseline remains Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **4**, total **8**, public SCC empty.  
**Production data cleanup:** the accidental second `Yuqiao Song` row (`cmu7yt0ax000ipe010t6b84f7`) was manually deleted only after a read-only check confirmed zero employee-config, year-opening and PayrollRun references; the first row (`cmu7ysrwk000gpe0102o1gp1y`) remains active. Both `PAYROLL_EMPLOYEE_CREATE` audit records were intentionally preserved.  
**Production verification:** after deployment the same finalized Jiancheng PayrollRun re-exported as a complete one-page LETTER PDF, closing the observed layout defect. Existing Yuqiao employee data became selectable and subsequent PayrollRun work proceeded normally. A future genuinely new employee creation remains the direct active check for the auto-select/field-clear behavior; no additional data mutation is required solely for verification.

### 2026-09-19 — Phase 9 8P-E Payroll operator workflow navigation

**Branch/State:** PR #2405 / final head `ba672c19` / squash `01e38a2a` / **MERGED / PR CI #5938 GREEN / POST-MERGE CI #5939 GREEN / DEPLOYED / PRODUCTION VERIFIED / CLOSED / NO MIGRATION**.  
**Observed usability problem:** production payroll entry required operators to scroll through employer/employee statutory setup, employer-level CRA remittance and employee Year Opening before reaching the recurring PayrollRun editor. This mixed low-frequency configuration and employer-level settlement with the high-frequency employee run workflow, increasing the chance of editing the wrong section or value.  
**Fix:** reorganize the existing Accounting Payroll Web adapter into three task/ownership-aligned workspaces behind a sticky employer/employee context bar: **Runs** is the default and contains recurring PayrollRun/pay-statement/net-pay work; **Employees** contains employer/employee maintenance, statutory config and Year Opening; **CRA** contains only employer-level CRA remittance. The employee selector is visibly non-operative in CRA view, and low-frequency statutory-config/Year Opening forms are collapsed behind explicit expand actions while current effective facts remain summarized. For `MONTHLY` employees, Runs also derives a transparent next-period suggestion from the latest POSTED run (`periodEnd + 1 day` through calendar month-end), leaves `payDate` operator-confirmed, and refuses to advance when the latest run is unfinished/reversed; manual date edits or loading an existing run stop auto-prefill for that employee session. Source characterization pins the default Runs workspace, sticky context, three-workspace separation and collapsed low-frequency editors, while focused pure tests pin period rollover/year-boundary/unfinished-run behavior.  
**Architecture/migration:** Web presentation/state composition only. Existing API routes, Payroll ownership, server calculation/finalization, YTD, Journal, employee-payment and CRA-remittance semantics are untouched. No Prisma/schema/migration, package/lockfile, public context edge, scanner allowance, direct-import baseline or compatibility change. Expected baseline remains Foundation **1** / External **1** / Identity **2** / Runtime **4**, total **8**, public SCC empty.  
**Production verification / closeout:** after deployment the operator verified all four targeted behaviors: (1) switching Jiancheng Song / Yuqiao Song updates the sticky context and run list without stale selected-run leakage; (2) both MONTHLY employees with latest POSTED period ending `2026-08-31` prefill `2026-09-01 -> 2026-09-30`, and a manual period edit is not overwritten; (3) Employees keeps statutory config and Year Opening as summary-first, intentionally expanded maintenance; and (4) CRA is visibly employer-scoped, disables employee scoping, and returns the same employer preview regardless of the previously selected employee. No synthetic PayrollRun, employee payment or CRA cash fact was created for UI verification. This UI follow-up is therefore closed. Overall Payroll 8P-E remains open only for its separate end-to-end closeout evidence; in particular CRA remittance settlement will be verified on the next real remittance rather than by paying early solely for testing, and posted-run reversal/correction evidence remains a separate future verification item.  
**Details:** `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/payroll/{page.tsx,payroll-setup-panel.tsx,payroll-year-opening-panel.tsx,payroll-run-panel.tsx,payroll-period-suggestion.ts,payroll-period-suggestion.test.ts,payroll-workflow-navigation.test.ts}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-9-payroll-design.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-19 — Identity / Staff last-active-admin atomicity hardening

**Local state:** latest `origin/dev@32aa6063` source baseline / **SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / LOCAL REVIEW APPROVED / REMOTE SUBMISSION AUTHORIZED / NO MIGRATION**. This Staff submission is isolated from the already-merged Phase 9 closeout/roadmap documentation.  
**Readiness/decision:** Phase 4 already assigned Staff persistence/business rules to Identity and explicitly deferred the non-atomic `active ADMIN count -> update` sequence. Repository review found both PostgreSQL advisory-lock patterns in Uber infrastructure and Prisma `Serializable` + `P2034` whole-transaction retry patterns in Coupons/Accounting. A normal transaction or per-target row lock is insufficient for the two-row write-skew race; the selected minimum is the existing Serializable/retry pattern, avoiding a new dependency, migration, lock-key namespace or owner change.  
**Implementation:** `StaffAdministrationService.updateStaff()` now performs target lookup, self-modification protection, role/status validation, last-active-admin predicate evaluation and User update inside one `Serializable` interactive transaction. `P2034` retries re-run the whole use case up to three attempts, so a concurrent demotion/disable winner forces the losing request to re-read current Staff state before it can commit. Existing `LAST_ACTIVE_ADMIN`, stable `userStableId` boundary, ADMIN/STAFF semantics, invite behavior and Admin controller 400/404 mapping remain unchanged.  
**Regression/architecture:** existing demotion characterization remains; coverage adds active-ADMIN disable protection, pins `Serializable` isolation, and models a serialization conflict followed by a retry that sees only one active ADMIN and returns `LAST_ACTIVE_ADMIN`. No context direction, direct-import debt, scanner allowance, public SCC, API response, Web UI, package/lockfile or Prisma/schema/migration change. Local lint/build/test were intentionally not run per repository workflow; GitHub Actions remains authoritative after remote submission.  
**Repository-wide final-tail audit:** read-only classification is complete and finds no additional modularization-blocking owner/boundary tail after this Staff atomicity batch. The older Admin Members `POST /admin/members/:userStableId/ban` path, ADMIN/STAFF-visible Member list, and arbitrary stable-user account-status operation are intentionally retained as development/test compatibility while the membership system still needs STAFF/ADMIN identities for end-to-end testing. They are not the canonical Staff-administration path and do not reclassify the Staff atomicity fix as incomplete. When membership testing no longer needs those identities, a separately reviewed cleanup should restrict Member list/status mutation to customer/member identities and retire the overlap. The two active Payment compatibilities remain provider/cutover gates; the Orders -> Loyalty/Membership concrete mutation seam is accepted as a bounded atomic transaction seam; POS/External module composition and owner-local Prisma/infrastructure imports are legitimate seams rather than numeric cleanup targets. The former Store temporary-close codec reverse edge and closed-compat annotation loophole are already closed. Uber Direct `order.paid.verified` remains a separately documented L3 durability/idempotency improvement candidate, while printer-agent packaging/no-op processor cleanup are ops/hygiene rather than owner-boundary blockers. Subject to reviewed PR CI for this Staff hardening, the repository-wide modularization tail is therefore fully dispositioned at the source/architecture level without forcing historical baseline counts to zero.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/auth/{staff-administration.service.ts,staff-administration.service.spec.ts}`, existing `apps/api/src/admin/staff/admin-staff.controller.ts` + controller characterization, `docs/architecture/{phase-4-identity-customer-benefits-messaging.md,current-dependency-graph.md}`, this worklog.
