### 2026-09-19 — Post-modularization P0 reliability / Uber Direct durable dispatch

**Local base/state:** latest `origin/dev@2007e30d` / branch `fix/uber-direct-durable-dispatch` / **SOURCE REVIEWED / PRODUCTION VERIFICATION PENDING / DIRECT REPLACEMENT / NO MIGRATION**.  
**Readiness:** Phase 5 already classified the private `order.paid.verified` Uber Direct path as a real L3 durability/idempotency tail outside the closed modularization program. The existing owner direction remains correct: Orders/Fulfillment owns dispatch intent/state and Deliveries owns provider protocol. Production read-only inventory on 2026-09-19 found zero existing Orders with `fulfillmentType=delivery` and `deliveryProvider=UBER`, so no historical dispatch backfill/migration or old/new compatibility route is required.  
**Implementation:** The private `OrderEventsBus/order.paid.verified` dispatch path is deleted and Orders durable dispatch becomes authoritative immediately. The durable `OpsEvent` journal records `requested -> attempt_started -> succeeded|failed|unknown -> reconciled`; the processor uses SKIP LOCKED claims and stale in-flight -> UNKNOWN recovery. One dispatch cycle performs the initial provider create plus up to three automatic retries (2s/5s/10s) only for `SAFE_TO_RETRY` outcomes; each current FAILED fact and its next automatic REQUESTED fact are persisted atomically. Definitive provider 4xx rejection including Direct rate-limit 429 is safe to retry, while local validation failures skip pointless retries and alert immediately; timeout/no-response/5xx/ambiguous responses are UNKNOWN and never automatically POST-retried. Final FAILED/UNKNOWN alerts reuse the existing Email-first / SMS-fallback Admin notification capability and include per-attempt reason/HTTP/error history. The ADMIN-only reconciliation page distinguishes FAILED from UNKNOWN and shows the same checkout-derived recipient/phone/address/instructions used by the dispatch request so an operator can create the delivery manually without hunting through another screen. FAILED may be retried from SanQ after fixing the cause (new cycle gets three safe automatic retries) or manually created in Uber Direct Dashboard and then bound back by `orderUuid`; UNKNOWN requires Dashboard verification by SanQ order number before bind/retry. The durable queue queries unresolved action-required facts directly, and reconciliation row-locks the Order before mutation so concurrent Admin actions serialize.  
**Compatibility / verification:** no Uber Direct active compatibility remains because the legacy dispatch route is deleted. Production verification still requires a real successful provider/local bind, safe-retry exhaustion with detailed alert evidence, UNKNOWN/reconciliation without duplicate courier creation, and manual Dashboard-create + SanQ bind recovery. Durable journal evidence is authoritative if best-effort alert delivery itself is interrupted.  
**Architecture/test effect:** no Prisma schema/migration, package/lockfile, public context direction or public SCC change. The central architecture scanner now forbids restoration of `OrderEventsBus/order.paid.verified`, pins the Orders-owned journal/processor/reconciliation boundary, safe retry allowance/backoff, SKIP LOCKED claim, UNKNOWN fail-closed semantics and ADMIN+MFA recovery. Focused specs cover provider classification, three safe automatic retries before alert, durable claim/stale recovery, provider-success atomic binding, UNKNOWN handling and explicit reconciliation. Per repository workflow, local lint/build/test are not run; reviewed GitHub Actions remains the authoritative execution gate.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/orders/order-delivery-checkout-metadata.ts`, `apps/api/src/orders/order-delivery-dispatch-*.{ts,spec.ts}`, `apps/api/src/orders/processors/order-delivery-dispatch.processor{,.spec}.ts`, `apps/api/src/orders/admin-order-delivery-dispatch.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/deliveries/{uber-direct.service.ts,uber-direct.service.spec.ts,uber-direct-dispatch.contract.ts,public-api.ts}`, `apps/api/src/messaging/templates/{zh-CN,en}/deliveryDispatchFailed.*.hbs`, `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/admin/(protected)/delivery-dispatch/**`, `apps/web/src/components/staff/AdminShell.tsx`, `tools/architecture/{context-baseline.json,scan-architecture.mjs}`, `docs/architecture/{phase-5-commerce-orders-fulfillment.md,active-compatibility-register.md,active-compatibility-register.json,current-dependency-graph.md}`, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`.

### 2026-09-19 — Post-modularization Accounting / Fantuan Adjustment detail evidence

**Final state:** PR #2424 / final head `1546a279` / squash `fead0039` / **MERGED / CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION**. This is a real-provider evidence correction after Phase 9 closeout; it does not reopen Phase 9 or alter the repository-wide modularization status.  
**Observed production evidence:** August Fantuan Summary materialized `Adjustment = +2694` cents and therefore correctly failed closed as `UNMAPPED_PROVIDER_COMPONENT`. The provider Detail workbook decomposes that amount by stable columns, not remarks: `Order type=Adjustment + Fee type=Compensation` is **+3354** cents and `Order type=Adjustment + Fee type=Deduction` is **-660** cents, net **+2694**. Ordinary Order rows independently reproduce the Summary Sales/Sales Tax/Commission totals, so neither Adjustment row belongs in Sales/HST.  
**Implementation:** Accounting Inbox accepts XLSX as stored `OTHER` evidence and a bounded Fantuan parser records only Adjustment rows. The Detail materializes as `FANTUAN / OTHER / fantuan:adjustment-detail:<period>` supplementary provider evidence; unknown Fee Types remain fail-closed. Settlement preview keeps the Summary as the only monthly write source, converts its generic Adjustment to control evidence, requires exactly one confirmed same-period Detail with exact net parity, then maps Compensation to `account_other_operating_revenue` and Deduction to `account_chargeback_adjustment_expense`. The Detail itself is NOOP for settlement writing. The expected-plan-hash authority conditionally includes the Detail identity/revision/review anchor and the Journal writer revalidates that evidence inside the same Serializable transaction before any write. Existing plans without supplementary evidence omit the new optional field, preserving historical hash/idempotency shape.  
**Architecture/dependency/test state:** no Prisma/schema/migration, new context edge, scanner allowance or provider wire change. Web changes only allow/display XLSX in the existing Accounting Inbox adapter. Focused source specs cover field-based XLSX parsing, upload routing, distinct account mapping, exact Summary/Detail parity, missing-detail blocking and transaction-time supplementary-revision conflict. The operator generated and pushed the pnpm 9.0.0 lockfile as head `6bafa9ad`; frozen-lockfile installation and the Architecture gate passed in PR CI #5992. That CI then failed only at API lint: 101 Prettier findings plus one `no-base-to-string` finding in the new XLSX parser, so later API build/strict/test steps were skipped while the independent Web job passed. The lint corrective was pushed as `3e1d955e`; PR CI #5993 then passed Architecture, API lint, API build, API strict declarations, all shared checks and the full Web job, but API tests exposed one focused parser-spec failure. The real Fantuan filename shape `...Details2026-08-01_2026-08-31...` caused the filename date regex `\b...\b` to miss the first date because `s` and `2` are both regex word characters; the parser therefore fell back to `Settle Time` and the wrong-period guard did not fire. The final parser fix replaced those word boundaries with numeric lookarounds `(?<!\d)...(?!\d)`, preserving the same period-validation rule while supporting the actual provider filename. Final head `1546a279` passed CI #5994 across Architecture, API/Web lint/build/strict/tests and was squash-merged to `dev` as `fead0039`. The explicitly authorized dependency remains `@keep-lts/xlsx@^0.18.6`.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-fantuan-adjustment-detail*.ts`, Accounting Inbox acquisition/provider-financial services, provider-settlement preview/policy/write-authority/Journal writer, Accounting Inbox Web upload, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-19 — Post-modularization Accounting / Settlement work-queue follow-up

**Local base/state:** post-#2424 `origin/dev` / branch `fix/accounting-settlement-net-total-display` / PR #2426 / **SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / REPLAY-GATE + CI LINT CORRECTIVE LOCAL / NO MIGRATION**. This is a Settlement usability/read-model follow-up after the merged Fantuan evidence correction; Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Observed production evidence:** the confirmed/replayed June Fantuan statement contains canonical `PAYOUT = 292539` cents (`Total transfer amount`) and the production Journal carries the same 292539-cent debit to `account_fantuan_pending`, while the Settlement card displayed `Net Total —`. The page also loaded only CONFIRMED Inbox documents, so before running Shadow Preview a posted statement looked identical to an unposted statement even though `accounting.provider_financial_document.v1` Journal evidence already existed.  
**Implementation:** the Settlement summary resolves canonical `component=PAYOUT` first and falls back to raw `Net Total` for Uber-compatible statements. A bounded ADMIN/ACCOUNTANT read-only `GET journal/provider-settlement/posting-states` projection reuses the existing settlement Journal query and returns `POSTED / NOT_POSTED + existingJournalEntryStableId` for at most 200 requested statement IDs. The Web page uses that projection to show only unposted `STATEMENT` documents in the default work queue, move posted statements into a collapsed audit-only history with Journal identity and no Replay action, and keep non-STATEMENT provider evidence (including Fantuan Detail) in a separate supporting/control-evidence section. Fresh post-write `ALREADY_POSTED` reconciliation updates the local projection immediately, moving the card out of the work queue. The first deployed Fantuan Detail upload also exposed an Inbox terminology ambiguity: the parser correctly suggested `PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_DOCUMENT / FANTUAN / documentType=OTHER`, but the UI labeled the Inbox classification as “结算单”, encouraging an operator to switch the recognized supplementary evidence to generic `OTHER_DOCUMENT`. The follow-up renames that Inbox class to `平台财务资料 / Provider financial evidence`, describes `documentType=OTHER` as supplementary evidence that is not independently posted, renames the confirmation action accordingly, and warns when recognized provider evidence is manually overridden to generic Other because that path does not materialize it for settlement matching. Manual override remains available. Focused API/Web tests pin posting-state projection, net-payout resolution, posted/pending bucketing and supporting-evidence exclusion. No parser, Journal writer, replay authority, financial semantics, Prisma/schema/migration, package dependency or cross-context edge change. The only route-set change is the explicitly authorized read-only posting-state endpoint inside the existing Accounting Provider Settlement controller. PR CI #6000 passed the complete Web job and the Architecture baseline, but API stopped at lint because the new posting-state query service spec did not match repository Prettier output; CI #6001 reduced that to one remaining Prettier expression in the same spec while the complete Web job stayed green. Production verification of the confirmed August Fantuan Summary + confirmed Detail then exposed a separate Web replay-gate compatibility gap: the Shadow Preview correctly produced Fantuan Pending 381311 cents and no provider-policy blocker, but the UI gate returned `EXPECTED_EXACTLY_ONE_PROVIDER_DOCUMENT` because it counted the supplementary Detail `NOOP` plan as a second replayable document. The local correction now counts only non-`NOOP` provider plans as actionable for one-document replay/completion, while any second READY/BLOCKED/ALREADY_POSTED provider document still fails closed; execution remains unchanged and continues writing only READY plans. Regression coverage pins both READY target + NOOP supporting evidence and ALREADY_POSTED target + NOOP supporting evidence. The remaining API spec expression is also reformatted exactly as CI #6001 requested. Per repository workflow, local lint/build/tests are not run; reviewed GitHub Actions remains the validation gate after user approval.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-provider-settlement-{controller,query.service}.ts`, controller/query specs, `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(site)/accounting/{contracts/settlements.ts,settlements/**}`, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-20 — Post-modularization Accounting / Human Review Revision authority

### 2026-09-21 — Post-modularization Accounting / Evidence Viewer Slice 1B logical file manager

**Final state:** PR #2436 merged to `dev` as `9ae4d85d`; CI #6042 API/Web green. User-generated additive migration `apps/api/prisma/migrations/20260921124637_add_accounting_evidence_folders/migration.sql` was committed as `cc4c8016` and reviewed against the schema: only the two new logical-folder tables, expected unique/index constraints and the artifact/folder foreign keys are present; there is no backfill, drop/rename, `storedUrl` mutation or physical-file migration. **DEV MERGED / CI GREEN / MIGRATION SQL REVIEWED / NO NEW DEPENDENCY**. Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Storage/ownership decision:** Accounting source binaries remain physically unchanged in the existing storage locations. In particular, this slice does not create nested `uploads/accounting/inbox/**` paths, does not move files on disk and does not rewrite `AccountingSourceArtifact.storedUrl`. Instead, additive `AccountingEvidenceFolder` + `AccountingEvidenceFolderAssignment` persistence owns operator-visible organization state outside the source artifact row. Absence of an assignment is the virtual **Unfiled** root; V1 intentionally supports first-level folders only and does not add folder rename/delete/nesting. One assignment per artifact enforces one current logical location.  
**Atomic file-manager authority:** new Accounting-owned file-manager service lists retained binary evidence by artifact stable ID, creates normalized case-insensitive folder names, and moves up to 100 selected artifact stable IDs atomically through the existing serializable Accounting writer. Moving to a folder upserts the assignment; moving to Unfiled removes it. Every created folder and every actual file move writes `AccountingAuditLog` before/after evidence. Confirmed/posted/provider artifacts are intentionally movable because folder assignment does not alter bytes, content hash, recognition evidence, Human Review, settlement or Journal authority. The API exposes only stable IDs; DB UUIDs remain internal.  
**Viewer UX:** `AccountingEvidenceViewer` adds **文件管理 / Manage files**. The manager provides All / Unfiled / folder filters, first-level folder creation, checkbox multi-select, select-visible, and a two-step **Move -> choose destination folder** action including Unfiled. The list is bounded to the newest 500 retained evidence artifacts and reports truncation rather than silently implying completeness. Existing preview/download/permanent-delete semantics remain independent; folder placement never grants deletion authority.  
**Migration:** completed by user-local generation as `20260921124637_add_accounting_evidence_folders`; SQL review confirms the expected additive-only shape. Existing artifacts require no backfill and remain Unfiled. No data rename/drop, `storedUrl` rewrite or physical-file migration is present.

### 2026-09-21 — Post-modularization Accounting / Document Recognition Slice 3V-A native-PDF verification

**Final state:** PR #2439 squash-merged to `dev` as `0d6909bb` after final head `8c67ffc4`; PR CI #6054 and merged-head CI #6055 API/Web green. **DEV MERGED / CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION / NO NEW DEPENDENCY / NO RUNTIME-PACKAGE CHANGE**. Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Native-text routing boundary:** adds Prisma-free `accounting-pdf-routing.ts` with a persisted, revalidated three-way decision: `USABLE_NATIVE_TEXT`, `SCAN_CANDIDATE`, or `FAIL_CLOSED`. The rule is Unicode-aware rather than ASCII-only, treats blank/fragment-only text as scan candidates, fails closed on truncated or materially suspicious native extraction, and records bounded quality/geometry metrics. Provider recognition/financial semantics run only for usable native text. Non-usable native text remains review evidence and cannot later bypass the gate through manual provider confirmation.  
**3V-A / 3V-B safety split:** this batch does not broaden the old scanned-PDF implementation. Only the historical completely blank `NO_NATIVE_TEXT` case may still use the existing whole-PDF synchronous Textract fallback; weak-but-nonblank scan candidates do not enter that path. The separate 3V-B slice remains responsible for bounded page inspection/rasterization, per-page synchronous Textract image OCR, page-aware geometry merge and whole-document completeness/failure semantics. Provider API fallback exclusion remains unchanged.  
**Provider layout hardening:** provider-financial parser advances to v5. Explicit `POPPLER / TEXT_ONLY` PDF evidence may resolve only same-line label/value amounts and records that exact line evidence; it no longer falls back to cross-line flattened named-amount adjacency. Provider statements whose label/value columns collapse into separate line groups therefore fail closed instead of recreating the historical Uber column-order defect. Geometry-backed extraction remains unchanged and continues to record immutable label/amount evidence.  
**Sanitized golden:** a test-only July Uber fixture is derived from the retained source as read-only reference; the original PDF is not committed or reprocessed. Identifiers are removed while the observed flattened `Sales / Tax on Sales / $2,603.36 / $338.48` shape, representative two-column geometry and control values are retained. The golden exercises `Poppler bbox normalization -> provider parser -> settlement control totals`, pins Sales **260336**, Tax on Sales **33848**, Net Total **143194**, excludes payout-section Sales, and requires all five Uber control-total checks to match. Coordinates are intentionally representative rather than byte-for-byte production floats, so the invariant is geometry/financial semantics rather than Poppler-version-specific coordinate text.  
**Architecture/scope:** `accounting-pdf-routing.ts` is added to the existing Prisma-free Accounting boundary guard. No Prisma schema/migration, package/lockfile, Docker/runtime package, provider wire contract, context direction, scanner allowance, public SCC, Web Clover/POS Clover, Phase 9 status, Expense->Journal, Sales Analytics or Trial Balance work changes in this slice. Per `AGENTS.md`, local lint/build/test commands are intentionally not run before user review; GitHub Actions remains the validation gate after explicit remote authorization.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-pdf-routing{,.spec}.ts`, Inbox acquisition + specs, provider parser/service + specs, `accounting-provider-financial-poppler-golden.spec.ts`, `test-fixtures/uber-july-native-poppler.*`, Prisma-boundary architecture spec, `docs/architecture/accounting-document-recognition-human-review-plan.md`, `ACCOUNTING_PRODUCT_ROADMAP.md`, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-21 — Post-modularization Accounting / Document Recognition Slice 3V-B scanned-PDF routing

**Final state:** PR #2440 squash-merged to `dev` as `0ac9117f` after final head `3c5c0400`; PR CI #6057 and merged-head CI #6058 API/Web green. **DEV MERGED / CI GREEN / PRODUCTION VERIFICATION PENDING / NO MIGRATION / NO NEW DEPENDENCY / NO RUNTIME-PACKAGE CHANGE**. Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Bounded local raster path:** `SCAN_CANDIDATE` PDFs replace the temporary raw-PDF Textract call with Accounting-owned `pdfinfo` page inspection plus sequential `pdftocairo` page rasterization using the already-installed `poppler-utils`. V1 production bounds are 6 pages, 200 DPI, 10-second Poppler command timeout, 20 MiB per raster output and the existing 25 MiB source-file ceiling. No Docker/package/lockfile change or Poppler pin is introduced.  
**Textract page adapter / authority:** each raster page is prepared as a non-cropped JPEG under the existing synchronous Textract 9.5 MB / 9000 px limits and sent sequentially through AnalyzeExpense. The scanned-PDF adapter consumes only LINE text/confidence/geometry and explicitly ignores page-level SummaryFields/line-item semantics for provider authority. Receipt-image AnalyzeExpense behavior remains unchanged. Lines are remapped to source PDF page IDs before provider parsing; an AWS_TEXTRACT geometry regression pins same-page provider label/value pairing.  
**Whole-document fail closed:** the merged PDF is limited to 2000 lines and 57,000,000 aggregate prepared-image bytes. Page-count overflow, Poppler failure/timeout/output overflow, image preparation failure, Textract error/timeout, zero-line or geometry-free page, page truncation, aggregate overflow or merged-line overflow aborts the whole OCR result. No partial prefix reaches provider parsing/materialization; the outer Inbox acquisition boundary records ParseRun ERROR while retaining SourceArtifact/PENDING_REVIEW evidence. When Textract is disabled, scan candidates remain local/manual review.  
**Routing/Provider API:** generic PDF review parse runs advance to v6 and store page-bounded `pdfOcrEvidence` separately from receipt `textractEvidence`; recognized provider routes carry the same OCR evidence into their recognition/financial parse runs so diagnostics are not lost on the early matched path. Provider API remains unchanged and currently exposes only CSV ingress; the scanned-PDF branch still excludes `PROVIDER_API`. Native usable PDFs stay Poppler-local and suspicious/truncated `FAIL_CLOSED` PDFs do not enter OCR. Historical Uber July evidence is untouched.  
**Architecture/scope:** `accounting-pdf-rasterizer.ts` and `accounting-scanned-pdf-recognition.ts` are added to the existing Prisma-free Accounting architecture guard. No Prisma/schema/migration, package/runtime dependency, context direction, scanner allowance, public SCC, Web Clover/POS Clover, Phase 9 status, Expense->Journal, Sales Analytics or Trial Balance work changes. PR CI #6057 and merged-head CI #6058 passed architecture, lint, build, strict declaration and API/Web test gates. Active production verification of the scanned-PDF route remains pending.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-pdf-rasterizer{,.spec}.ts`, `accounting-scanned-pdf-recognition{,.spec}.ts`, Textract page adapter/spec, Inbox acquisition/spec, provider geometry regression, Prisma-free architecture guard, `docs/architecture/accounting-document-recognition-human-review-plan.md`, `ACCOUNTING_PRODUCT_ROADMAP.md`, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`, `docs/architecture/current-dependency-graph.md`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-21 — Post-modularization Accounting / Document Recognition Reliability Slice B Expense reconciliation + booking correction audit

**Source state:** **MERGED / PR CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION / NO NEW DEPENDENCY** through PR #2443 / squash `6e89bc3b`. Phase 9 remains `PRODUCTION VERIFIED / CLOSED`; this is post-modularization Accounting correctness work.  
**Expense extraction / reconciliation:** ordinary Expense recognition now carries `financialConsistency = MATCHED / MISMATCH / INSUFFICIENT` across native Poppler PDF, bounded scanned-PDF Textract-page OCR and image Textract paths. Native PDF expense values use Accounting-owned bbox geometry to pair labels with inline or same-row/right-side amounts; inline values must start with money, preventing descriptive text such as `Total discounts of $185.00` from becoming a generic Total. The Bell-style regression pins `Taxes = 974`, `Total current charges = 8469`, derived subtotal `7495`, and prevents the flattened `$185` HST false match. `MISMATCH` lowers extraction confidence and the Inbox/Expense UI surfaces a prominent fail-visible warning instead of a misleading HIGH result. Foreign-source values reconcile in source currency and are not numerically compared to CAD booking totals.  
**Expense operator correction:** machine extraction remains immutable/read-only. The existing Expense booking fields are the editable operator authority and are now explicitly presented as **Final booking values**. Date, source currency, total, category, subtotal and HST can be corrected directly from the source evidence; Web highlights fields whose final values differ from machine-observed values. There is no separate Expense DRAFT/CONFIRMED review workflow.  
**Audit / posting safety:** final booking still must balance exactly before Expense creation. Confirmation writes the machine snapshot, final booked values, machine consistency state, corrected-field list, operator and timestamp to `ExpenseDocument.extractionJson.bookingReview` and to a `CONFIRM_EXPENSE_BOOKING` Accounting audit entry. FX differences do not count as CAD amount corrections. Provider Financial Human Review remains unchanged.  
**Schema / migration:** the originally prototyped Expense Review enum/table/API was removed before merge. Final Slice B has **NO PRISMA SCHEMA CHANGE / NO MIGRATION REQUIRED**.  
**Architecture:** all extraction, correction display, booking validation and audit policy remains inside the existing Accounting L3 boundary. No package/lockfile, external-provider wire contract, context direction, direct-import allowance, architecture scanner baseline or public SCC change is introduced.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting B2-P0 Canonical SALE continuity hardening

**Source state:** **P0A PRODUCTION VERIFIED / P0B PRODUCTION VERIFIED + COMPLETE / NO MIGRATION / NO GRAPH CHANGE**. P0B merged through PR #2459 from final head `a7daaf9b` as squash `2c4cb834`; CI #6118 passed Architecture and all API/Web lint/build/strict/tests. Phase 9 remains closed.  
**P0A production evidence:** the operator used the existing expected-plan-hash guarded canonical SALE replay for Toronto business coverage `2026-09-13..2026-09-23` exclusive. Preview/execution reported 131 candidates / 131 READY / 0 BLOCKED, zero parity delta and balanced debit/credit of 213172 cents. Read-only production verification after execution found 131 immutable `order.financial_sale.v1` facts, 131 exactly-one canonical SALE Journal anchors, zero missing anchors, zero duplicate anchors and per-day fact/Journal count parity from 2026-09-13 through 2026-09-22.  
**P0B root cause / runtime design:** the 5D-B2B cutover established guarded replay/write authority but intentionally left `AccountingCanonicalSalePostingService.postCanonicalSale()` without a production runtime caller, so new immutable Orders facts could accumulate without Journal convergence. `AccountingCanonicalSalePostingProcessor` now runs under Accounting. Startup performs a full accounting-start-to-now reconciliation in bounded seven-day chunks; steady state polls a 48-hour recovery window every 15 seconds and re-runs full reconciliation every 24 hours. Each run batch-reads existing SALE Journal anchors, writes at most 25 missing eligible facts through the existing canonical posting service, and leaves an incomplete full pass eligible for the next poll so a backlog drains without being falsely marked reconciled.  
**Authority / recovery rules:** automatic posting is restricted to `IMMUTABLE_SALE_SNAPSHOT`; historical `LEGACY_CURRENT_ORDER` stays on the controlled replay path. POS/Web facts use the existing canonical posting policy. Uber facts fail closed to statement authority while Accounting provider coverage lacks `liveOrderFactCutoverAt`, and only facts at/after a configured cutover become auto-postable. A provider-coverage read failure defers/fails only affected Uber facts and does not block POS/Web convergence. Existing Journal idempotency/source-anchor checks remain the retry and multi-instance convergence authority; no second queue/table or owner write-back is added.  
**Architecture / verification:** the architecture regression pins the processor as the only production caller of `postCanonicalSale()`, requires Orders/Store access through their public boundaries and forbids processor Prisma/deep Orders access. No Prisma schema/migration, package dependency, Orders implementation mutation, provider wire behavior, Web/POS Clover path, Journal policy or public context direction changed. Forward verification is now complete: Web Order `c6ab16o6d7urm906lohrep6yg` paid at `2026-09-22T15:09:16.738Z`; immutable SALE fact persisted at `15:09:16.846Z`; exactly one canonical Journal `journal_qc0l2z5vzakcg6nd2fzybwdw` was created automatically at `15:09:27.551Z` with `canonical-sale:c6ab16o6d7urm906lohrep6yg:v1`, four lines and debit=credit=1495 cents. Processor logs at `15:09:27.661Z` record `mode=RECENT`, `scanned=23`, `immutable=23`, `alreadyPosted=22`, `posted=1`, `blocked=0`, `failed=0`, `complete=true`, proving no manual replay was required. Uber remains deferred until its separately controlled live-order cutover.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-canonical-sale-posting.processor{,.spec}.ts`, `accounting.module.ts`, `canonical-financial-facts-boundary.architecture.spec.ts`, `ACCOUNTING_PRODUCT_ROADMAP.md`, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`, `docs/architecture/current-dependency-graph.md`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting B2-A Sales Analytics contracts + attribution policy

**Source state:** **MERGED / CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION / NO GRAPH CHANGE** through PR #2461; final head `8f982260` passed CI #6125 and squash merged as `1c1549c2`. Initial CI #6124 reported only six Prettier formatting failures, which were corrected without semantic changes. B2-P0B is production-verified independently and Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Orders attribution boundary:** adds `ORDER_SALES_ATTRIBUTION_READER` and `OrderSalesAttributionModule`. The V1 public DTO is deliberately non-monetary: `sourceFactStableId`, `orderStableId`, `storeStableId`, `occurredAt`, `channel`, canonical `primaryPaymentMethod`, source evidence and primary-payment evidence only. Direct `order.financial_sale.v1` attribution prefers immutable SALE evidence; if no immutable SALE exists, historical dimensions fall back explicitly to `LEGACY_CURRENT_ORDER`. Immutable `order.financial_adjustment.v1` / `order.financial_reversal.v1` attribution preserves the change fact's source identity/channel/time but inherits canonical primary payment method from the original SALE attribution rather than interpreting before/after tender state. Missing original SALE attribution stays missing, and malformed immutable evidence fails closed. No `*Cents`, `Order.totalCents`, change monetary state or payment-breakdown amount crosses this boundary.  
**Accounting policy:** adds an explicit canonical Sales/provider source-fact allowlist before account mapping, then a pure Journal account -> Sales component/sign policy for gross sales, discounts, delivery, surcharge, output tax, tip/other operating revenue and provider costs; this prevents unrelated Expense/Payroll/Manual Journals using shared accounts from leaking into Sales Analytics. A separate Journal tender policy maps Store Cash-equivalent, Clover Card, Uber/Fantuan receivable and Store Balance movements without pretending Store Cash can distinguish CASH from WECHAT_ALIPAY. Attribution evidence is classified as `IMMUTABLE / LEGACY_CURRENT_ORDER / MISSING`; provider financial coverage is fail-visible as `COMPLETE / INCOMPLETE / UNKNOWN / NOT_APPLICABLE`. The approved management formula is preserved exactly: `Channel Contribution = Net Sales Revenue - Platform Commission - Payment Processing Fees - Platform Promotion Expense`; Tips, Other Operating Revenue, Advertising, Chargeback and provider-other-fee remain separate metrics.  
**Runtime / architecture scope:** current `GET /accounting/report/slice`, Sales UI and Dashboard remain unchanged in B2-A. No Accounting report authority moved in this slice. The Orders contract is exported only through `orders/public-api`; its reader owns Orders persistence internally. Accounting references Orders only through the existing public direction. No Prisma schema/migration, package dependency, Journal posting rule, provider wire behavior, scanner allowance, SCC or Web contract change was introduced.  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/orders/order-sales-attribution.{contract,service,module}.ts`, `order-sales-attribution.service.spec.ts`, `orders/public-api.ts`, `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-sales-analytics.policy{,.spec}.ts`, `accounting-sales-analytics-boundary.architecture.spec.ts`, roadmap/backlog/dependency graph and this worklog.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting B2-B Canonical Journal Sales projection

**Source state:** **MERGED / CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION / NO GRAPH CHANGE** through PR #2462; final head `645f0423` passed CI #6130 and squash merged as `fe9b08da`. Earlier CI rounds exposed only lint/type-safe test-fixture cleanup and the existing Accounting controller vertical-boundary route/capability registration required by the new endpoint; no production authority was relaxed. B2-P0B is production-verified independently and Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Canonical projection:** adds read-only `GET /accounting/report/sales?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD`. Date-only inputs use the configured Store timezone, clamp to canonical `accountingStartDate`, use an exclusive next-day Journal bound and reject reversed or >370-day ranges. The projection reads only active configured-store Journals whose `sourceFactType` is in the B2-A Sales/provider allowlist, verifies source owner semantics, and derives every monetary metric exclusively from Journal lines. It returns overall and daily summaries, by-channel and canonical-primary-payment summaries, actual Journal tender mix, source-type breakdown, Orders-attribution quality and provider-coverage evidence.  
**Attribution / historical replacement:** direct SALE/change amounts remain Journal-owned and use only the public `ORDER_SALES_ATTRIBUTION_READER` for dimensions. Missing Orders dimensions are retained under `UNATTRIBUTED` with `MISSING` evidence rather than reconstructed from Order money. Provider statement Journals resolve provider identity from Accounting-owned `AccountingProviderFinancialDocument`; missing document identity fails closed. Uber pre-cutover replacement reversals resolve their original active SALE Journal anchor, require ORDER / `order.financial_sale.v1` authority, then reuse the original Order attribution. This leaves the historical accounting equation visible as original SALE + explicit reversal + provider-statement replacement rather than mutating history.  
**Coverage / tender semantics:** provider coverage remains explicit `COMPLETE / INCOMPLETE / UNKNOWN / NOT_APPLICABLE`; missing Clover coverage with CARD/Clover activity is therefore UNKNOWN rather than zero-fee. Uber/Fantuan coverage rows remain incomplete until Accounting records completeness. Tender mix is derived only from Journal balance-side accounts (`STORE_CASH_EQUIVALENT / CLOVER_CARD / UBER_EATS / FANTUAN / STORE_BALANCE`) and stays distinct from primary-payment revenue attribution.  
**Read-only production sanity evidence:** applying the same B2-A source/account signs directly to June 2026 production Journals yields 428 canonical Sales/provider Journals (342 SALE + 1 order reversal + 83 Uber historical replacement reversals + 2 provider documents), Gross Sales 1,082,721c, Discounts 4,755c, Output Tax 132,122c, Platform Commission 209,923c and Platform Promotion 60,800c. This confirms the projection set includes explicit Uber replacement/reversal history rather than only mutable Orders. P0B forward verification was rechecked in the same read-only session and still had no post-deploy paid Order to test.  
**Compatibility / architecture:** legacy `GET /accounting/report/slice`, `OrderReportingFactsModule`, Sales UI and Dashboard remain untouched for B2-C/B2-D consumer cutover. The new service reads Accounting persistence through `ACCOUNTING_DB`, Orders only through `../orders/public-api`, Store through its public reader and coverage through the existing Accounting query service. No schema/migration, package, Journal posting mutation, provider wire change, scanner allowance or new context direction is introduced. Focused unit and architecture regressions cover Journal authority, historical Uber replacement, missing-dimension fail-visible behavior, broken Accounting references, date clamping and legacy-route coexistence. Per repository workflow, local lint/build/tests are not run before user review.  
**Details:** `accounting-sales-analytics.{contract,service}.ts`, `accounting-sales-analytics.service.spec.ts`, `accounting-sales-analytics-projection.architecture.spec.ts`, `accounting-reports.controller.ts`, `accounting.module.ts`, roadmap/backlog/dependency graph and this worklog.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting EFA-B2 canonical Expense v2 posting

**State:** **MERGED / CI GREEN / NO MIGRATION / NO GRAPH CHANGE** through PR #2469 / `6674ab1cdd6bcba78998698cde69469c40b0b03d`; CI #6150 passed. Predecessor EFA-B1 is merged through PR #2468 / `2250b22d`, PR CI #6145 passed, and user-generated migration `20260922183548_accounting_efa_b1_funding_attribution_foundation` is committed to `dev` as `3e445345`; production application remains a later gate. Sales B2 remains production-verified/closed and Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Posting authority:** adds `CanonicalExpenseFactV2` without changing the historical v1 fact/key contract. v2 authority is rebuilt from persisted ExpenseDocument/Split rows plus persisted funding-account facts; every split carries `splitStableId/category/amount/tax/paidFromAccountStableId`. Funding accounts must be active CAD `ASSET` accounts of operational type `CASH/BANK/PLATFORM_WALLET`. A v2 document that still owns legacy document-level `AccountingExpensePaymentAllocation` rows fails closed, while incomplete split funding remains unposted.  
**Grouped Journal behavior:** v2 splits are grouped by reviewed funding account and produce one balanced Journal per account. Each group debits the relevant operating-expense category lines, debits that group's recoverable HST/GST, and credits the reviewed funding account for the group total. Idempotency is account-scoped and deterministic: `canonical-expense:<documentStableId>:funding:<accountStableId>:v2`. The full group set is written through the existing caller-owned Serializable Expense transaction; no second transaction or generic Journal bypass is introduced.  
**Hardening/tests:** the Expense-specific Journal writer now accepts discriminated v1/v2 authority, revalidates persisted v2 split identity/funding/account prerequisites immediately before write, rejects v1/v2 source-fact-version crossover or stale funding-group anchors once any canonical Expense anchor exists, and reuses existing accounting-start/period-lock/idempotent replay behavior. Focused regressions cover same-account split grouping, mixed-account 1:N posting, missing funding, invalid funding account, legacy-allocation rejection, period lock, identical retry and source-authority drift. The architecture guard pins the v2 source-fact/idempotency seam while retaining the v1 contract.  
**Boundary / deferred at B2 close:** B2 itself did not activate current Expense writes or Web/PWA row-level funding; that cutover belongs to EFA-C. Management-only filtering remains EFA-D. Historical `AccountingExpensePaymentAllocation` remains for v1 authority. No Prisma schema/migration, package/dependency, provider path, context direction, direct-import allowance, scanner baseline or SCC change is added by B2.  
**Details:** `accounting-expense-journal.policy.ts`, `accounting-expense-journal-write-authority.ts`, `accounting-expense-journal-posting.service.ts`, `accounting-journal.service.ts`, focused policy/posting/write/architecture specs, EFA design, roadmap, dependency graph and this worklog.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting EFA-C Expense write/UI cutover

**State:** **MERGED / CI GREEN / PRODUCTION DEPLOYED + VERIFIED / NO NEW MIGRATION / NO GRAPH CHANGE** through PR #2470 / final head `483109e6` / squash merge `42e25b04`; CI #6155 passed API/Web/Architecture gates. EFA-B2 remains merged through PR #2469 / `6674ab1cdd6bcba78998698cde69469c40b0b03d` with CI #6150 green; Sales B2 remains production-verified/closed and Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Readiness / production gate:** the original pre-deploy audit found the B1 migration unapplied and production still on three v1 Expenses. That gate was subsequently cleared: `20260922183548_accounting_efa_b1_funding_attribution_foundation` applied successfully at `2026-09-22 22:51:15Z`, production moved to the EFA-C/D runtime, and new Expenses persisted as v2 split-funded records without legacy document-level allocations. The original three v1 Expenses remain intact with their historical allocation authority.  
**Write / completion cutover:** current manual Expense, unified Inbox confirmation and pending-document confirmation now persist `fundingAttributionVersion=2` and write each split's nullable `paidFromAccountId`; they create no document-level payment allocation. Runtime rejects stale document-level `accountStableId` / `paymentAllocations` write payloads. Funding accounts are validated as active CAD operational ASSET accounts. A null split funding value deliberately leaves a confirmed v2 Expense unposted. The new audited, period-locked `PUT /accounting/expenses/:documentStableId/split-funding` completion requires every split exactly once, permits identical replay, rejects replacement/posted mutation and delegates final 1..N grouping/posting to the existing B2 authority. Historical v1 `payment-allocations` completion remains available and is explicitly v1-only.  
**Read/UI compatibility:** Expense query/presentation dual-reads v1 allocations and v2 split funding. Payment-account filters match either authority; `UNASSIGNED` means no v1 allocation or any unfunded v2 split, while `ASSIGNED` requires a v1 allocation or all v2 splits funded. Manual Expense and Inbox final booking rows now carry category / pre-tax / tax mode / HST / payment account / delete directly; the standalone v2 payment-allocation card is removed and new rows inherit the previous funding account. Inbox Quick Classify rows also carry payment account, Enter/Next item inherits category/tax/payment account, and aggregation keys by category + payment account to preserve funding attribution. Repeated use of one account across several splits is valid and Web performs no Journal grouping. Records summarize v2 funding from splits and route unposted v2 records into split-level completion while retaining the v1 editor for historical records.  
**Account policy configuration / EFA-D boundary:** Accounting Settings now sends `includeFundedExpensesInManagementReports` when creating an account and can view/toggle/save the flag for existing active operational accounts through a narrow Accounting-owned route. No Dashboard/P&L/category/trend/export arithmetic reads the flag in EFA-C; canonical Journal, account movement, actual cash flow and HST behavior remains unchanged pending EFA-D.  
**Architecture/tests:** no Prisma schema/migration, package/dependency, Journal schema/policy redesign, JournalLine funding dimension, Orders/Payments/Clover/Uber change, provider path, context direction, direct-import allowance, scanner baseline or SCC change is introduced. Focused source regressions cover the v2 write contract, same-account split funding, v1/v2 completion separation, dual account/ASSIGNED/UNASSIGNED query semantics, route inventory and Web row-level funding. PR CI initially exposed one Web parser typo, then API Prettier formatting and finally the existing ExpenseSplit-writer ownership guard; all were corrected in-scope, with split funding completion moved behind `accounting-expense-split.writer.ts`. Final CI #6155 passed all required API/Web/Architecture gates before squash merge `42e25b04`.  
**Details:** `accounting-expense.{contracts,controller,service,query}.ts`, Expense split writer/query/completion/architecture specs, Accounting Chart policy route/service, Expense/Inbox/Settings Web surfaces, `docs/architecture/accounting-expense-funding-attribution.md`, roadmap/backlog/dependency graph and this worklog.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting EFA-D Management reporting cutover

**State:** **PRODUCTION VERIFIED / CLOSED / NO NEW MIGRATION / NO GRAPH CHANGE** through PR #2471 / final head `ddb01f74` / squash merge `2d3abc0e`; CI #6158 passed API/Web/Architecture gates. Production verification completed on `main@2d3abc0e`; Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Readiness/result:** all Management expense surfaces already converge through `AccountingFinancialReportsService`: Dashboard and P&L call the same Journal projection, Management/Boss CSV/PDF delegate to P&L, and category/trend rows are derived from those projected facts. Raw transaction CSV also reused that projection but is explicitly canonical under the EFA scope matrix; account balance/movement and actual cash flow already use separate canonical Journal queries. Therefore EFA-D can remain inside the existing Reporting owner with no route, Web contract or context-edge expansion.  
**Scope cutover:** `readProjection()` now requires explicit `MANAGEMENT` or `CANONICAL` scope. Dashboard/P&L use Management; raw transaction CSV uses Canonical. Only canonical Expense v2 Journal groups (`EXPENSE_DOCUMENT` + `accounting.expense_document.v2` + source-fact version 2) inspect the group's single operational funding credit line. If that account has `includeFundedExpensesInManagementReports=false`, the group's P&L Expense facts are omitted from Management projections; null/default/true includes them. Historical Expense v1 and all non-v2 facts remain unchanged. A malformed v2 group with zero or multiple operational funding credit lines fails closed instead of silently changing management arithmetic.  
**Canonical/statutory preservation:** recoverable GST/HST remains canonical because Journal input tax is accumulated before the Management Expense-fact filter. Raw canonical transaction CSV still includes excluded v2 Expense facts. Account balance/account movement and actual cash flow remain on their independent canonical query paths and do not read the management flag. Management/Boss exports inherit P&L scope without new export-specific logic.  
**Regression/architecture:** characterization pins an included v2 Expense, an excluded v2 Expense, a historical v1 Expense on the same excluded account, Management P&L/category arithmetic, canonical transaction-export retention and canonical Dashboard input-tax arithmetic. No Prisma/schema/migration, package/dependency, Journal schema, JournalLine funding dimension, API route/response contract, provider path, context direction, direct-import allowance, scanner baseline or SCC change is introduced. Production migration/deployment gates passed with `20260922183548_accounting_efa_b1_funding_attribution_foundation` applied at `2026-09-22 22:51:15Z`; the three historical Expenses remain v1 with balanced legacy Journals. Production verification then passed all required v2 cases: included `expense_nha2w24tprp8s74921k3ge9p` -> one balanced `3212c` Primary Bank Journal; excluded `expense_o5uf67it43suw1c4sk06jz3e` -> one balanced `9039c` CIBC Journal with canonical Expense/HST/account movement retained and `0c` Management Expense; mixed `expense_ramw3wdzp4r0wkxndshpnyz2` -> two balanced account-scoped Journals (`1174c` CIBC + `4102c` Primary Bank = `5276c`). Both newly verified v2 documents have zero legacy `AccountingExpensePaymentAllocation` rows. For the excluded + mixed verification population, canonical Expense=`13214c`, recoverable HST=`1101c`, cash/account movement=`14315c`, Management Expense=`4097c`, and excluded CIBC-funded Expense=`9117c`. **EFA is PRODUCTION VERIFIED / CLOSED; B3 Trial Balance / Balance Movement is ready for readiness audit without reopening Phase 9.**  
**Details:** `apps/api/src/accounting/accounting-financial-reports.{service,characterization.spec}.ts`, `docs/architecture/accounting-expense-funding-attribution.md`, `ACCOUNTING_PRODUCT_ROADMAP.md`, `POST_MODULARIZATION_BACKLOG.md`, `docs/architecture/current-dependency-graph.md`, this worklog.

### 2026-09-22 — Post-Modularization Accounting B3-A canonical Trial Balance core

**Branch/State:** `feat/accounting-b3a-trial-balance-core` from `origin/dev@1182a46e` / **B3 READINESS COMPLETE / B3-A SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / LOCAL REVIEW PENDING / CI NOT YET RUN / NO MIGRATION EXPECTED**. The baseline head passed GitHub Actions CI #6168 before this branch. Phase 9 remains closed.  
**Readiness evidence:** read-only production inspection found 1,501 canonical Journal entries / 4,897 lines, debit=credit=`7,798,968c`, zero unbalanced entries, zero `OPENING_BALANCE` Journals, 29 CAD accounts, zero non-CAD Journals/accounts and zero period-close rows. Account-class reconstruction yielded Assets=`2,104,938c`, Liabilities=`816,109c`, Revenue=`3,687,507c`, Expense=`2,398,678c`, Direct Equity=`0c` and cumulative recorded earnings=`1,288,829c`, reconciling `Assets - Liabilities - Earnings = 0`. Seven current Journals have null `storeStableId`, all Expense-document Journals, so B3 v1 is intentionally whole-ledger rather than store-filtered.  
**Implementation:** adds `accounting-trial-balance.contract.ts`, a pure `accounting-trial-balance.policy.ts`, `AccountingTrialBalanceService`, focused policy/service/architecture regressions and AccountingModule provider registration. The service reads only canonical `AccountingJournalLine -> AccountingJournalEntry/AccountingAccount` data from Accounting persistence, starts the read window at configured `accountingStartDate`, clamps report start in the Store business timezone, defaults to per-currency CAD scope, retains inactive historical accounts, classifies explicit `OPENING_BALANCE` Journals into opening balances, attaches month/year close visibility and fails closed on per-entry or opening/period/closing imbalance. ASSET/EXPENSE are debit-normal; LIABILITY/EQUITY/REVENUE are credit-normal, so contra-revenue remains negative without account-specific hacks.  
**Boundary:** B3-A does not reuse P&L `MANAGEMENT` projection/filtering; therefore EFA-excluded Expense v2 Journals remain canonical Trial Balance facts. It adds no HTTP route, Web/PWA contract, CSV/PDF export, Balance Movement UI, schema/migration, store filter, FX conversion, Orders/Payments/Loyalty/Uber dependency, scanner allowance or old `report/account-balance` contraction. Expected context graph is unchanged. The approved continuation is `B3-B HTTP/public contract -> B3-C Balance Movement projection -> B3-D production reconciliation/closeout`; B4 retains UI/export/drill-through polish.  
**Validation:** per repository rules, no local lint/build/Jest/scanner command is run before user review. Remote PR/CI validation is still pending.

## Rule for future entries

For each modularization code batch, append exactly one chronological entry before
local review. The entry must include:

1. date;
2. Phase / Slice / work-package name;
3. PR number and reviewed SHA once available, otherwise the local branch/state;
4. concise description of the ownership/boundary/compatibility change;
5. measurable architecture effect when applicable (for example dependency debt or
   compatibility state change);
6. the highest status actually reached (`SOURCE`, `CI`, `DEPLOYED`, `VERIFIED`, or
   `CLOSED`);
7. links/paths to the detailed phase, dependency, compatibility, payment, migration,
   or verification documents.

When CI, deployment, or active verification happens after the source entry was
written, update that same entry's state/evidence rather than adding a misleading
second implementation entry. A later genuinely new slice gets a new entry. Under
the 2026-09-06 verification cadence, a normal modularization Slice may stop at
`MERGED / CI GREEN` without its own production active-test cycle; record the affected
runtime behaviors as Phase-level verification scope. Immediately before Phase closeout,
add/update the Phase closeout entry with the consolidated deployment/active-verification
plan and evidence, and only mark the Phase `PRODUCTION VERIFIED / CLOSED` after it passes.
